2. <a id="step-2-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates-item-2"></a>**Get a police clearance certificate (PCC).** MOI's work visa needs a police clearance (good conduct certificate) from your home country, attested by the Qatar Embassy <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>.

   You'll need:
   - a passport valid at least 3 months <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>;
   - to live at your present address, which must be the permanent or emergency address in the passport <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>;
   - a scan of the passport data page attested by a first-class gazetted officer (by the Bangladesh Embassy if you are abroad) <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>;
   - an attested copy of your NID, birth certificate or ward councillor certificate, if your MRP (machine-readable passport) shows no address <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.

   1. Open pcc.police.gov.bd, register, log in and press Apply; fill in personal details, then addresses, upload the scans, check and submit; nothing can be changed afterwards <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.
   2. Pay Tk 1,500 by treasury challan, code 1-7301-0001-2681, at any Bangladesh Bank (the central bank) or Sonali Bank branch, or online by card with a service charge <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - Is the fee Tk 1,500 (the portal) or Tk 500 (Bangladesh Police's guidance and the Embassy in Doha) <sup>[81](#o81), [94](#o94), [95 (2021)](#o95)</sup>?

   3. The police verify you at your present address; track it by texting "PCC S <reference number>" to 26969 <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>.
   4. Collect it with a copy of your passport or application <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>. If you apply from abroad, someone else can collect it if you upload, with the application, an attested authorisation letter naming them and their NID number <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>. Certificates are issued in English <sup>[94](#o94)</sup>.
   5. From Qatar, apply online yourself or through a nominated relative; the police station of your permanent address in Bangladesh issues it <sup>[95 (2021)](#o95)</sup>.
   6. Have it attested by the Ministry of Foreign Affairs in Dhaka, then the Qatar Embassy ([Step 2, item 3: Get your certificates attested](#step-2-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates-item-3)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [8](#o8)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How long does a police clearance take to process?
   > - How long does a police clearance stay valid for Qatar?

   **If it fails:** the PCC helpdesk, Sunday to Thursday 9 am to 5 pm, 01320001824, 01320001825, help.pcc@police.gov.bd <sup>[81](#o81)</sup>; Dhaka Metropolitan Police one-stop service, 01320037339, 01320037329 <sup>[96](#o96)</sup>.

3. <a id="step-2-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates-item-3"></a>**Get your certificates attested.** MOI's visa needs education certificates attested for the profession <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>, and QVC asks for your academic qualification certified by the authorities in Qatar and Qatar's MOFA (Ministry of Foreign Affairs) and finalised by the local embassy, with the original <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>. The QVC appointment letter gives the full list <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>. Qatar is not a party to the Hague Apostille Convention (under which member countries accept an apostille certificate instead of embassy legalisation), so a Bangladeshi apostille, including Bangladesh MOFA's five-day e-Apostille with a QR code, does not replace attestation for Qatar <sup>[97](#o97), [98](#o98), [99](#o99)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Are workers with no certificates asked for one <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>?

   1. Get the issuing body's verification first: SSC and HSC (Secondary and Higher Secondary School Certificate) by the Education Board, then the Ministry of Education; degrees by the university, then the Ministry of Education <sup>[100](#o100), [51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>. A driving licence goes to a notary and BRTA (the Bangladesh Road Transport Authority, which issues licences), then the Law Ministry <sup>[100](#o100), [101](#o101)</sup>; a passport copy, experience, character or NID papers to a notary, then the Law Ministry <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>; birth, nikah and marriage certificates to the Law Ministry <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.
   2. Then every document needs attestation by Bangladesh's MOFA <sup>[100](#o100)</sup>. MyGov (mygov.bd, the Bangladesh government's online services portal) lists MOFA's "Certificate attestation for foreign-bound citizens" service, and you check an application's status at mygov.bd (application-latest-status) <sup>[102](#o102), [103](#o103)</sup>.
   3. Then the Qatari mission legalises it: QVC Dhaka has a document attestation counter that handles Qatar Embassy legalisation through service providers <sup>[35](#o35), [104](#o104)</sup>.
   4. From Qatar, the employer or you can instead use Qatar MOFA's "Complete Attestation Outside Qatar" e-service at mofa.gov.qa (an e-services account is needed) or the Consular Affairs building, Sunday to Thursday 7:30 to 13:00, which sends the documents to the Qatari mission in Bangladesh <sup>[104](#o104), [105 (2024)](#o105)</sup>. It costs QR 150, plus the equivalent of QR 120 for the mission, plus Bangladesh MOFA's fees <sup>[104](#o104)</sup>. Qatar MOFA attests a degree only if the university is on Qatar's Ministry of Education list of accredited universities <sup>[104](#o104)</sup>.
   5. In Qatar, the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha attests education, marriage, birth and police clearance certificates already attested by MOFA in Dhaka: QR 37 (regular) or QR 55 (express) for the first 5 pages, QR 8 per extra page <sup>[106](#o106)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - How do you apply for MOFA attestation (not the apostille) <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>?
   > - What is MOFA attestation's fee per document, and how long does it take <sup>[102](#o102)</sup>?
   > - What does a real MOFA attestation look like <sup>[99](#o99)</sup>?
   > - What are the Qatar Embassy in Dhaka's own fee, hours and documents <sup>[98](#o98)</sup>?

   **If it fails:** MOFA Bangladesh document authentication, Segunbagicha, Dhaka 1000, +8802-223381852, dgcnw@mofa.gov.bd <sup>[103](#o103)</sup>; MOFA Qatar, Government Contact Center 109 (24 hours) or the Attestation Department, 40111188 <sup>[104](#o104)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa"></a>

### 3.4 Step 3: The employer gets your work visa

This happens in Qatar, between the employer, MOL and MOI <sup>[7 (2023)](#o7), [8](#o8)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-1"></a>**The employer gets MOL's recruitment approval.** The company applies online; it is free and needs an active establishment ID with no ban <sup>[7 (2023)](#o7)</sup>. If MOL approves the gender, number and profession of workers, the request goes to a nationalities selection system and then to MOI <sup>[7 (2023)](#o7)</sup>. A new company on the fast track gets at most 10 labour visa approvals on its first request <sup>[29 (2022)](#o29)</sup>. The employer tracks the application with its number, year and establishment ID; MOL's phone is 16505 or 44068979 <sup>[107 (2022)](#o107)</sup>. Companies applying for a group visa need the Permanent Committee for Recruitment's approval of the nationality and number of workers, and pay QR 300 <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-2"></a>**The employer gets the visa from MOI.** The visa costs QR 300 <sup>[8](#o8)</sup>; MOI needs education certificates attested for the profession, a police clearance attested by the Qatar Embassy, an employment contract and a medical test passed outside Qatar <sup>[8](#o8), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. QVC Bangladesh lists four work visa types: Work Visa - Multi Entry, Work Visa - Single Entry, Work Yearly Residence and Work Yearly Visit <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-3"></a>**The employer puts the contract into MOI's system.** When the employer applies through MOI's "Issue Visa" service it enters the work contract; it then updates and signs it in the "Examination Procedures outside Country" e-service and pays QR 490 <sup>[79 (2021)](#o79)</sup>. For nationalities set in the visa system, the visa cannot be printed or used until both worker and employer have signed the contract <sup>[79 (2021)](#o79)</sup>. The contract is made in MOL's multilingual e-contract system; its legal form and language are under Your rights at work (see Your contract) <sup>[109 (2022)](#o109)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-4"></a>**The Bangladesh Embassy in Doha attests the papers.** The Embassy receives demand letters Sunday to Thursday, 7:30 to 14:00 <sup>[31 (2021)](#o31)</sup>. For an individual male worker, the sponsor or its PRO can have the contract attested there with copies of the worker's passport and visa and the company's details, for QR 50, ready after three working days, and gets back the attested contract <sup>[88 (2021)](#o88)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-5"></a>**The visa must be used in time.** A visa issued in advance must be used within 90 days, and nobody may enter Qatar on any visa before his fingerprints are taken <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does MOL's recruitment approval take <sup>[7 (2023)](#o7), [8](#o8), [87](#o87)</sup>?
> - How long does MOI's visa take <sup>[7 (2023)](#o7), [8](#o8), [87](#o87)</sup>?
> - How long does the QVC result take <sup>[7 (2023)](#o7), [8](#o8), [87](#o87)</sup>?

<a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical"></a>

### 3.5 Step 4: Qatar Visa Center: contract, fingerprints and medical

At QVC your documents are checked, your fingerprints, iris and face are recorded, you sign the employment contract if it applies, and you take the medical <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>. In Bangladesh it is run by SQ Services Limited, and MOI processes the results, which MoPH checks <sup>[35](#o35), [87](#o87)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-1"></a>**Book the appointment** on qatarvisacenter.com, which MOI's Visa Services page links to <sup>[35](#o35), [83](#o83)</sup>. The worker or the employer books with the visa reference number (VRN) <sup>[N4 (2018)](#n4), [N30](#n30)</sup>. Passport validity: [Step 2, item 1: Get a passport valid for more than 195 days](#step-2-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates-item-1) <sup>[84](#o84)</sup>. Your appointment letter gives the date, the centre's address, the fees to pay and the full list of documents to bring <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What does QVC's booking form ask for?

   **Where:** QVC Dhaka, Rupayan Trade Center (11th floor), 114 Kazi Nazrul Islam Avenue, Bangla Motor, Ramna, Dhaka-1000; +880 9666 777 101 (Sunday to Thursday, 08:30 to 17:00); info.bgd@qatarvisacenter.com <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>. Fingerprints and photos 08:30 to 16:30, medical 09:00 to 17:00, Sunday to Thursday <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>; its 2026 holiday closures (such as Eid-ul-Adha and Victory Day, 16 December) are listed on its site <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does QVC Sylhet take appointments, given that QVC's pages list a Sylhet centre open Sunday to Thursday, 8:30 to 16:30 <sup>[36](#o36), [84](#o84)</sup>, while the Ministry said in May 2026 that the only centre is in Dhaka <sup>[24](#o24)</sup>?

   **If you miss it** the status shows "Appointment No show" and you must book again <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>; for a missed or earlier appointment the optional walk-in service takes applications without a booking, but seats are limited <sup>[34](#o34), [35](#o35)</sup>.

2. <a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-2"></a>**Bring:**
   - the printed appointment letter and your original passport <sup>[34](#o34), [36](#o36)</sup>;
   - your academic qualification, attested as in [Step 2, item 3: Get your certificates attested](#step-2-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates-item-3), with the original <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>;
   - any previous Qatar ID and Qatar medical papers <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>;
   - your glasses or contact lenses, any specialist's report on a medical condition, and earlier TB (tuberculosis) records and X-rays if you were ever treated for TB <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.

   Bring no phones or other electronic gadgets, bags (only a plastic bag of papers), sealed envelopes, flammable or sharp items: there is nowhere to store them <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>. Only applicants may go in; under-16s need a guardian, and people over 60, people with a physical disability and hearing-impaired people (with an interpreter) may bring a companion <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>. Arrive 15 minutes early, and don't fast before the medical <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.

3. <a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-3"></a>**At the centre** (allow about 2 hours for the medical <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>):
   1. Hand in the appointment letter, your passport and documents for checking, and take a token <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.
   2. At the biometric booth give your fingerprints, iris scan and face photo, and sign the employment contract if it applies <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>; you are given the contract to review and sign electronically <sup>[N4 (2018)](#n4)</sup>. MOI has said you are handed a copy of the contract at the end <sup>[N4 (2018)](#n4)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - Are workers still handed a copy of the contract at the end <sup>[N4 (2018)](#n4)</sup>?

   3. At the medical facility register with the same token; a nurse takes measurements and a blood sample, gives a vaccination and records your eyesight; then an X-ray and a doctor's examination, after which you may leave <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>.

4. <a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-4"></a>**Fees.** MOI, not QVC's operator, collects the biometric visa fee <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>, and the employer pays the QR 490 contract fee ([Step 3, item 3: The employer puts the contract into MOI's system](#step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-3)) <sup>[79 (2021)](#o79)</sup>. The lounge (Tk 4,260 in Dhaka) and other extra services are optional; the sponsor pays for the lounge when booking online, and it does not speed up the visa <sup>[34](#o34), [35](#o35)</sup>. All fees, once paid, are non-refundable <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - May any QVC fee be charged to the worker, given that QVC told applicants in Pakistan in 2025 that appointments are free and the employer pays for the visa <sup>[N30](#n30)</sup>?

5. <a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-5"></a>**The medical.** What QVC does is in [Step 4, item 3: At the centre](#step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-3) <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>. Qatar's Medical Commission (MoPH's Medical Commission Department) uses, in Qatar, a fitness form that tests a chest X-ray, creatinine (kidney), HIV, hepatitis B and C, syphilis and TB <sup>[110 (2020)](#o110)</sup>, asks you to declare and sign whether you have epilepsy, asthma, psychiatric disorders or hearing problems <sup>[110 (2020)](#o110)</sup>, records blood pressure, pulse, height, weight and eyesight, and is marked FIT or UNFIT <sup>[110 (2020)](#o110)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does QVC test for the same conditions?
   > - Do Qatar-bound workers need only the QVC medical, not WAFID testing (the screening, by the Gulf Health Council, the regional body that coordinates the Gulf states' health policies, of Gulf work and residence visa applicants at accredited medical centres in their home countries, formerly GAMCA), given reports that they are exempt while WAFID's booking form still lists Qatar as a destination <sup>[N87](#n87), [111](#o111)</sup>?

6. <a id="step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-6"></a>**Track your application** at www.qatarvisacenter.com/cnt/trackapp ("Track Application") with your passport number, visa number and the captcha <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>. The statuses are Appointment Confirmed, Employment Contract Status, Biometric Enrolment Complete, Medical Test Complete, Medical Test Status and Results Available with Employer <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>. "Biometric Completed" means your fingerprints, iris and face are enrolled <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>; "Medical Complete" means the reports have gone to MoPH to be checked, and you may be called back for a re-test or sent to local doctors for more tests <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>; "Result Available" means MOI has processed everything and the employer can see the visa result <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>; "Re-Enrolment Required" means go back to redo your biometrics and contact QVC at once <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>. Finishing the biometrics or medical does not mean a visa is issued: ask your employer for the decision <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>. A referral status can stay "pending with authorities" for 10 to 25 days <sup>[N88 (2023)](#n88)</sup>.

   **If you are found "unfit":** officially MoPH checks the reports and QVC may call you back or refer you for more tests <sup>[87](#o87)</sup>. In practice the worker isn't given the report: the employer says the visa was cancelled as "medically unfit", and QVC answers a complaint with "contact your sponsor" <sup>[N28 (2023)](#n28), [N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>. The sponsor gets only the status from MoPH, never the report, but can ask QVC in Qatar about the rejection <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>. Workers report hepatitis B, HIV, hepatitis C, sexually transmitted disease and scars from past TB as common reasons for "unfit", and high blood sugar as a reason for a referral and re-test within 30 days <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29), [N89 (2024)](#n89), [N90 (2023)](#n90)</sup>. After an "unfit" result the employer can apply for a new visa and the worker repeats the QVC process; one worker was told to pay the new fee himself <sup>[N31 (2022)](#n31)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the official list of conditions that make a worker unfit?
   > - Is there any appeal against an "unfit" result?
   > - How long does a QVC result stay valid (forum users say 60 to 90 days) <sup>[N29 (2023)](#n29)</sup>?

<a id="fake-qvc-agents"></a>

#### 3.5.1 Fake QVC agents

QVC offers no jobs of any kind and warns against people posing as its agents <sup>[35](#o35)</sup>. Never share your VRN, passport number or personal details with anyone claiming to be QVC on social media, phone, SMS or WhatsApp; QVC staff can't "fix" a medical result <sup>[N30](#n30)</sup>. QVC helpline: +974 44069999 in Qatar, +880 9666 777 101 in Bangladesh <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>. If an agency took money for the visa or medical, complain as in "Free visa" or a bought visa, item 2 <sup>[17](#o17), [91](#o91)</sup>.

<a id="step-5-check-the-visa-yourself"></a>

### 3.6 Step 5: Check the visa yourself

1. <a id="step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1"></a>**On MOI's Visa Inquiry.** It follows up work visa applications and shows issued visas, which you can print or reprint online; printing fees vary by visa type <sup>[83](#o83), [85 (2022)](#o85)</sup>.
   1. Open MOI's portal, Inquiries > Visa Services (portal.moi.gov.qa/wps/portal/MOIInternet/services/inquiries/visaservices), and choose Visa Inquiry; the form is at fees2.moi.gov.qa/moipay/visa/inquiry?language=en <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.
   2. If it opens in Arabic, choose رقم السمة (visa number) or رقم جواز السفر (passport number) <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>. A search by application number (رقم الطلب) also needs the sponsor's Qatar ID (QID) number and the application date <sup>[112](#o112)</sup>.
   3. Type the number, select the nationality, type the image code (ادخل الرمز) and press استعلم (Inquire) <sup>[112](#o112), [85 (2022)](#o85)</sup>.
   4. Read the latest status; for a rejected application the Remarks icon shows the reasons <sup>[85 (2022)](#o85)</sup>. A valid result shows your name, nationality, passport number, date of birth, gender, visa type, stay duration, issue date, validity, visa status, passport validity and sponsor details <sup>[N80](#n80)</sup>. "Valid To Use" means issued and you can travel; "Ready To Print", approved but the sponsor must pay; "Under Process" or "Under Outside Process", not finished; other statuses include "Rejected", "Visa Expired" and "Cancelled"; "Sorry, Requested Visa Not Found" means not processed yet or wrong details typed <sup>[N80](#n80)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - What is MOI's own description of the result fields and statuses <sup>[85 (2022)](#o85)</sup>?

   In Metrash (MOI's app, [Step 7, item 6: Register on Metrash](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-6)), choose Visa, then visa status inquiry, and enter the application details <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>.

2. <a id="step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-2"></a>**At BMET.** BMET checks job visas and demand letters free within 3 working days, and checks Qatar visas online; it also has a visa-check app on Google Play <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>. Bring the visa and, where it applies, a copy of the passport page with the visa stamp to BMET in Dhaka or your district employment and manpower office <sup>[17](#o17), [44](#o44)</sup>. Ami Probashi, a digital platform for Bangladeshi migrants, checks Qatar visas on its helpline 16768 for a small fee and answers valid or invalid by SMS <sup>[N92](#n92)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What web address or app name does BMET use for its visa check <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>?

3. <a id="step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-3"></a>**Check it is a work visa.** The work visa types QVC Bangladesh handles are listed in [Step 3: The employer gets your work visa](#step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa) <sup>[34](#o34)</sup>. A Hayya e-visa never allows work <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>; its checker, hayya.qa/en/visa-inquiry, takes the Entry Reference Number, or passport number and nationality <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>. For Hayya visas sold as work visas, see [Tourist visa: Hayya A1](#tourist-visa-hayya-a1), Scams (for switching a visit visa to work, see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[N62 (2023)](#n62)</sup>.

4. <a id="step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-4"></a>**Check the dates.** For nationalities set in MOI's visa system, the visa can't be printed or used until you and the employer have signed the contract ([Step 3, item 3: The employer puts the contract into MOI's system](#step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-3)) <sup>[79 (2021)](#o79)</sup>, and you must enter Qatar within 90 days of its issue ([Step 3, item 5: The visa must be used in time](#step-3-the-employer-gets-your-work-visa-item-5)) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

Qatari government websites end in gov.qa; look-alikes such as gov-qa.com are used for phishing <sup>[N93](#n93)</sup>. **If the visa is fake or rejected:** call MOI on 2367111 or email info@moi.gov.qa <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>; complain about the agency as in "Free visa" or a bought visa, item 2 <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>. Buying or selling a Qatar visa is a crime there (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is there any appeal or re-application route if MOI rejects the visa, beyond the reasons Visa Inquiry shows <sup>[85 (2022)](#o85)</sup>?

<a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight"></a>

### 3.7 Step 6: Orientation, BMET clearance, insurance, a loan and the flight

1. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-1"></a>**Do the compulsory 3-day pre-departure orientation (PDO)** at one of 70 training institutes: apply on OEP and pay the institute's fee against a money receipt <sup>[17](#o17), [44](#o44)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the click path for booking the PDO on OEP?

2. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-2"></a>**The contract before clearance.** In BMET's posted letter for BTS Overseas (RL2434), for 100 women housemaids <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>, the agency must sign the contract with each worker before clearance, submit the demand letter or power of attorney and skill certificates to BMET before the smart card is issued, and give each worker an attested Bangla translation of the contract <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does BMET set the same conditions for company workers?

3. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-3"></a>**Get BMET clearance (the smart card).** The smart card is your emigration clearance; it also makes you a member of the Welfare Board (WEWB), whose members get a school admission quota and scholarships for their children, financial help when sick, loans on easier terms from Probashi Kallyan Bank (PKB, the government bank for migrant workers) and Tk 3 lakh to the heir if the member dies; and it gives you migrant insurance ([Step 6, item 4: Know your insurance](#step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-4)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [114](#o114), [44](#o44)</sup>.
   1. **If an agency recruited you,** it applies after the visa with its application, the visa copy, the attested contract (where applicable), an attested copy of your registration card, its undertaking on job certainty on Tk 300 non-judicial stamp paper, the original bank pay order for the welfare and smart card fees, and the original source income-tax challan; BMET's emigration branch clears within 3 working days <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.
   2. **If you got the visa yourself,** apply at BMET's emigration branch in Dhaka or the Chattogram district employment and manpower office with your application, the visa copy, the contract (if any), an attested copy of your registration card, a declaration on Tk 300 stamp paper and pay orders for the welfare and insurance fees; 3 working days <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.
   3. **Fees:**
      - welfare fee: Tk 5,500 from 1 October 2026 <sup>[82](#o82)</sup>;
      - insurance premium: Tk 1,000, once <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>;
      - clearance fee: Tk 250 per worker <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>;
      - source income tax: Tk 400 (single visa, not attested) or Tk 250 (attested), and Tk 1,200 (skilled) or Tk 800 (unskilled) on a group visa <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>;
      - smart card fee: listed as Tk 250 for workers who got the visa themselves <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - Did the insurance premium change with the welfare fee rise <sup>[115](#o115)</sup>?

   **Check it is real** at portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>. Welfare desks at Dhaka, Chattogram and Sylhet airports check smart cards before departure <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>. **If it goes wrong:** BMET's Director (Emigration), +8802222222415, 01711-135901, diremi@bmet.gov.bd; then the Additional Director General (Employment), 02-48321949, adgemp@bmet.gov.bd <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>; then the ladder in "Free visa" or a bought visa, item 7 <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.

4. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-4"></a>**Know your insurance.** Workers who leave with BMET clearance on or after 12 March 2023 are insured by Jiban Bima Corporation (JBC, which insures migrant workers under a contract with the Welfare Board, effective 1 January 2023) for Tk 10 lakh, for 5 years from the smart card's date, for workers aged 18 to 55, for the one-time Tk 1,000 <sup>[44](#o44), [117 (2024)](#o117)</sup>. It pays on death, permanent disability, and job loss with return within 6 months of going (job loss is not covered for a worker who went entirely at the employer's cost); what it pays and how to claim are under If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job ([If a worker dies, item 7: The insurance: Tk 10 lakh](#if-a-worker-dies-item-7); If you are injured or fall ill, item 5) <sup>[117 (2024)](#o117), [44](#o44)</sup>. You can download your insurance certificate from JBC's e-service <sup>[117 (2024)](#o117)</sup>.

5. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-5"></a>**If you need a loan,** PKB lends up to Tk 3 lakh at 9% simple interest a year, repaid in equal monthly instalments over up to 3 years including a grace period, and decides within 7 working days of complete papers <sup>[43](#o43), [118 (2019)](#o118)</sup>. Women migrants have a separate loan on the same terms <sup>[119](#o119)</sup>. You must be 18 to 60 and not in default on any loan, and you and your guarantors must live permanently in the branch's area <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>. A worker going back on a re-entry visa can also borrow, adding proof of remitting through legal channels, proof of employment abroad and a statement of migration costs <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>:
   - the free application form;
   - 3 recent photos;
   - copies of your NID, passport, visa and BMET card, and the contract if you have one;
   - 3 signed MICR (magnetic-ink, machine-readable) cheque leaves from your own active bank account, with its statement;
   - a PKB savings account opened with at least Tk 500;
   - two guarantors, each with a photo, an NID copy and proof of permanent address <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>:
     - one financially solvent person, with 3 signed MICR cheques, a statement and proof of income <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>;
     - one family member <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

   1. Apply at the PKB branch for your permanent address, or online on mygov.bd, uploading your photo, passport, visa, BMET card and NID and your guarantors' NIDs and photos; you can apply only once until the loan is approved or refused, and you pay nothing when you submit <sup>[43](#o43), [118 (2019)](#o118)</sup>.
   2. After the decision, pay processing and documentation fees of 0.5% each plus VAT, and Tk 900 per lakh for risk cover, plus stamps <sup>[43](#o43), [118 (2019)](#o118)</sup>.

   **If it goes wrong:** PKB helpline 16238, +880 9677-787878 from abroad <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>; loans department 01716-175560, migration@pkb.gov.bd <sup>[120](#o120)</sup>; the grievance officer, GM (Admin and Accounts), +88-02-48321195, gm2@pkb.gov.bd (30 working days), then dmd@pkb.gov.bd, then md@pkb.gov.bd <sup>[120](#o120)</sup>.

6. <a id="step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-6"></a>**The flight.** Who pays the airfare is under Who pays what (see [Before paying: what the law allows](#before-paying-what-the-law-allows)) <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>. In BMET's posted letter for BTS Overseas (RL2434), an agency representative flies with each group or meets it at the Qatar airport <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>. Before you go, check you have <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>:
   - a valid passport;
   - the work visa;
   - the medical certificate (where it applies);
   - the demand letter;
   - the contract attested by the destination country's embassy;
   - the agency's name, address and phone;
   - the BMET clearance or smart card;
   - the air ticket.

   Keep one set of copies of all papers at home and at least 3 with you <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>. Never carry a packet for anyone whose contents you don't know: drug offences abroad can carry the death penalty <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.

<a id="step-7-after-you-arrive"></a>

### 3.8 Step 7: After you arrive

1. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-1"></a>**Take the medical and give fingerprints within 30 days.** Anyone entering on a residence visa must take the medical within 30 days of entry, then give fingerprints, then complete the residence procedures; the employer must let you go to the authorities for this, and you may not refuse <sup>[37](#o37), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. You may not stay more than 30 days without passing the medical <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. At the Medical Commission, bring your original passport, a clear copy of your residence permit with a mobile number for emergencies, and the medical certificate from the accredited centre at home; the fee is QR 100 by card, and the result goes to MOI electronically <sup>[121 (2021)](#o121)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Who pays the QR 100 for company workers <sup>[121 (2021)](#o121)</sup>?

2. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-2"></a>**Make sure the employer issues your residence permit (RP) and QID.** The RP is carried on the QID card, and the employer alone is responsible for your residency <sup>[38 (2021)](#o38), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.
   1. After your medical and fingerprints, the employer issues the RP online in MOI's e-services (Residency > Issue/Reactivate RP by Visa Number); companies get the card by Qatar Post <sup>[38 (2021)](#o38)</sup>.
   2. For a first company RP MOI needs the labour contract attested by the labour department, the signed form, your original passport, a copy of the company's establishment ID, a photo and a blood group certificate <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>. The RP costs QR 1,000 a year plus QR 100 for the card, and the work permit QR 100 a year <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - May the worker be charged any of these fees?

   3. Track the application on Hukoomi (hukoomi.gov.qa, Qatar's government services portal), "Residence Permit Application Tracking": enter the visa number and the sponsor's ID number, type the verification code and click "Submit"; it is free <sup>[89 (2021)](#o89)</sup>.
   4. Once you have the QID, check its expiry on Hukoomi, "Inquire about the Expiry Date of Official Documents": choose "ID Number" and type it, or "Passport Number" and your nationality, enter the code and click "Search"; free <sup>[123 (2021)](#o123)</sup>.
   5. The sponsor must renew the RP within 90 days of its expiry; late steps cost QR 10 a day, up to QR 6,000 <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [49 (2015)](#o49), [37](#o37)</sup>.

      > **Need to know**
      > - Can the worker be fined for a late renewal, given that an MOI service page says the expatriate renews and is fined <sup>[37](#o37)</sup>?

   **If the company doesn't issue your QID:** ask its mandoob (PRO), manager or HR (human resources department); if it still doesn't, take your visa and passport to the Public Prosecution's residency affairs office inside the CEID (Search and Follow-up Department) building and complain against the company <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>. An RP that needs MOI's Expatriate Affairs approval (because of passport expiry, fingerprint or medical problems) can be applied for free in Metrash: Residency > Residency procedures > Residence Permit <sup>[124 (2022)](#o124)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What are the Public Prosecution's residency affairs office's official name and address <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>?

   **If you can't find your company on arrival,** take copies of your visa and passport to MOL's Labour Relations Department (Sanaiya Street 13, or Al Huda Tower opposite City Center) or the nearest police station, or ask the Embassy's labour wing; the Search and Follow-up Department or the Public Prosecution's residency affairs office can give you the employer's number <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Are these offices and addresses, given by the Embassy in 2021, still current <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>?

   **If you lose your QID,** ask the PRO to apply for a replacement through Metrash; otherwise go to any immigration (Jawazat) service centre with your original passport and a copy of the QID <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.

   **If you get a new passport** (after expiry, loss or damage), the RP is moved to it: bring the old and new passports, the form and, if the passport was lost, a loss report; it is done in Metrash, at MOI service centres or at the Passport headquarters and costs QR 100 <sup>[125](#o125)</sup>.

3. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-3"></a>**Keep your passport.** The employer must give it back once the residence procedures are done, unless you ask in writing for it to be kept, and must hand it over whenever you ask <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>; keeping it against this rule is punishable by a fine of up to QR 25,000 <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

4. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-4"></a>**Get your contract.** Open MOL's "Portal for Printing Employment Contracts" on Hukoomi (hukoomi.gov.qa/en/services/request-ecopy-of-employment-contract), enter your QID or visa number and mobile number, then the code sent by SMS, and click "Login"; it is free <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>. The contract is often not online, so collect a hard copy from the company's PRO, manager or HR <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>. If the job or pay differs from the contract, the dispute goes first to MOL for amicable settlement (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 2) <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>; one worker promised a welder's job at QR 2,500 was put on a desert farm at QR 800 <sup>[N39 (2022)](#n39)</sup>.

5. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-5"></a>**Get a SIM card.** At Vodafone Qatar a prepaid line needs your passport or a valid QID and a postpaid line a QID; documents are checked electronically and prepaid activation takes up to 2 minutes <sup>[127](#o127)</sup>. Its My Vodafone App sells a SIM or eSIM after checking your passport or QID by face recognition, and delivers a physical SIM free <sup>[128 (2023)](#o128)</sup>. At Ooredoo a Hala (prepaid) SIM costs QR 35, or is free from its eShop, with 30 days' validity; a top-up of QR 10 or more within the 30 days keeps the line <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>. Once you have the QID, put the line under it: Metrash registration needs the number under your own QID with the QID expiry updated, and after a new connection or a change of ownership you wait a week before registering <sup>[127](#o127)</sup>. Ooredoo charges QR 50 to transfer ownership, free if you update your QID and register for Ooredoo Mobile Money <sup>[129](#o129)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What are the CRA's (Communications Regulatory Authority, Qatar's telecoms regulator) rules on how many lines one person may hold <sup>[N94 (2023)](#n94), [129](#o129)</sup>?
   > - Must a line registered on a passport move to the QID <sup>[N94 (2023)](#n94), [129](#o129)</sup>?
   > - What is Ooredoo's document list for a new Hala line <sup>[N94 (2023)](#n94), [129](#o129)</sup>?

6. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-6"></a>**Register on Metrash,** MOI's free app with more than 400 services (residence, visas, traffic) and an e-wallet of your official documents, in Arabic, English, French, Spanish, Hindi and Urdu (no Bangla) <sup>[130](#o130)</sup>. You log in with your QID number and password, which needs a registered account <sup>[124 (2022)](#o124)</sup>; the new Metrash replaced Metrash2 on 1 March 2025 <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>. To register you need a valid QID, an active mobile number and a smartphone <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>:
   1. Install it from Google Play or the App Store, and check the publisher is Qatar's Ministry of Interior <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>.
   2. Choose registration or activation and enter your QID <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>.
   3. Complete the mobile verification and set security options <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>.
   4. If activation fails, check your QID details and registered mobile number, then contact MOI <sup>[N91](#n91), [127](#o127)</sup>.

   Hukoomi support: contact@hukoomi.qa, +974 44069999 <sup>[124 (2022)](#o124)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What are MOI's official Metrash registration steps?

7. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-7"></a>**Get your wages into a bank account.** If you are on a monthly or annual wage, it is due on the 1st of each month and must be paid within seven days into your account at a financial institution in Qatar through WPS <sup>[90](#o90), [2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>; other workers' wages are due every two weeks <sup>[90](#o90)</sup>. At QIB (Qatar Islamic Bank), for example, the employer opens the salary account for workers earning up to QR 5,000 <sup>[131](#o131), [N95](#n95)</sup>:
   1. The employer enters your QID and mobile number on the bank's WPS portal and uploads your ID documents <sup>[131](#o131), [N95](#n95)</sup>.
   2. You get an SMS to download the QIB Lite app, verify your identity and activate the account <sup>[131](#o131), [N95](#n95)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does a QCB (Qatar Central Bank) directive still require banks to open WPS salary accounts for all workers whatever their salary, forbid them to refuse and allow at least five free salary withdrawals a month, as reported in 2015 <sup>[N96 (2015)](#n96)</sup>?
   > - Can a worker open a salary account before getting the QID <sup>[131](#o131)</sup>?

8. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-8"></a>**Get a health card** at the nearest health centre of PHCC (Primary Health Care Corporation) with your valid QID, a 4x3 cm photo and a utility bill as proof of address; it costs QR 100, by card only <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>. It gives subsidised care at PHCC centres and Hamad Medical Corporation (HMC, Qatar's main public hospital group), and subsidised medicines at government pharmacies <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>. By law health insurance is compulsory for residents, no RP is issued or renewed without proof of cover, and the employer pays its workers' premiums <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Is the insurance law's residents' phase enforced in practice?

9. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-item-9"></a>**If you left without BMET clearance,** you are not automatically a Welfare Board member, as workers with a clearance card are; you can join from Qatar (see [Welfare Board sign-up if you left without BMET clearance](#welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [116](#o116)</sup>.

<a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance"></a>

#### 3.8.1 Welfare Board sign-up if you left without BMET clearance

Its member benefits are listed in [Step 6, item 3: Get BMET clearance (the smart card)](#step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-3) <sup>[114](#o114)</sup>. Workers who left with a BMET clearance card are members automatically <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [116](#o116)</sup>.

1. <a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance-item-1"></a>**Check first** at [portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check](https://portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check) whether you already have clearance or membership <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.
2. <a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance-item-2"></a>**Apply online** at [portal.wewb.gov.bd](https://portal.wewb.gov.bd/), and print the information page that appears after a successful application <sup>[116](#o116), [114](#o114)</sup>.
3. <a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance-item-3"></a>**Take it to the Embassy** with your passport, a Qatar ID copy and proof of legal work: Bangladesh Embassy, Building 153, Street 820, Zone 43, New Al-Hilal, Doha, during the counter hours under Contacts (see [In Qatar](#in-qatar)) <sup>[116](#o116), [51 (2021)](#o51), [133](#o133)</sup>.
4. <a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance-item-4"></a>**Pay** the equivalent of Tk 6,500 in riyals, life insurance included <sup>[116](#o116)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What riyal amount does the Embassy counter charge now, given that its own pages say QR 140 and QR 170, both set before the welfare fee rose on 1 October 2026 <sup>[114](#o114), [51 (2021)](#o51), [82](#o82)</sup>?

5. <a id="welfare-board-sign-up-if-you-left-without-bmet-clearance-item-5"></a>**What you get back:** a membership card with a QR code, which can be checked at www.member.wewb.gov.bd <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does membership taken from Qatar expire <sup>[116](#o116), [114](#o114)</sup>?
   > - How is it renewed <sup>[116](#o116), [114](#o114)</sup>?

If something goes wrong, contact the Embassy's labour wing, +974 4467 1499, labour.doha@mofa.gov.bd <sup>[92](#o92)</sup>; Probash-Bondhu, on 16135 in Bangladesh or +8809610102030 from abroad <sup>[65](#o65)</sup>; or the Board's complaints focal person, 02-222220976, ad.pro@wewb.gov.bd, or online at www.wewb.gov.bd <sup>[134 (2024)](#o134)</sup>.

<a id="scams"></a>

### 3.9 Scams

Complain about an agency to BMET as in "Free visa" or a bought visa, item 2, and about an employer in Qatar to MOL (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 2) <sup>[17](#o17), [4](#o4)</sup>.

<a id="done-to-workers"></a>

#### 3.9.1 Done to workers

**"Free visa" or a bought visa**
Someone sells you a visa for Tk 4 to 7 lakh so you can work for whoever you find, saying work is easy to find once you are in Qatar; workers find no job and face arrest or deportation <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [N8](#n8)</sup>. Protection: [Step 1, item 5: If a relative or friend in Qatar sends you a visa](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-5), including having the offer checked at BMET <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>; the penalties are covered separately (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

It is illegal in both countries ([Step 1, item 5: If a relative or friend in Qatar sends you a visa](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-5)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [28](#o28)</sup>. Have it checked, with the other papers, at BMET in Dhaka or the district employment and manpower office <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.

**If the agency cheats you** (takes money and doesn't send you, or sends you to a different job): under Bangladesh's Overseas Employment and Migrants Act 2013, taking money on a false promise of a job abroad, withholding a migrant's passport, visa or papers without lawful reason, or luring someone with false promises of higher pay carries up to 5 years' prison and a fine of at least Tk 1 lakh <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>; publishing an overseas job advert without government or BMET approval carries up to 1 year and at least Tk 50,000 <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>. Migrants and people cheated in the name of migration have a right to reasonable legal aid <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>.

1. <a id="done-to-workers-item-1"></a>**Write down the facts:** who took the money, from whom, when and how much; a Ministry joint secretary said in 2025 that action follows when these are given <sup>[N65](#n65)</sup>.
2. <a id="done-to-workers-item-2"></a>**Complain to BMET.** Address a self-explanatory application with your proof to BMET's Director General and file it at the workers' complaint cell; it is free and meant to be settled within 30 working days <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>. BMET forms an inquiry committee, holds a hearing and recommends a decision to the Ministry, which decides <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.
3. <a id="done-to-workers-item-3"></a>**Or complain to the Ministry's enforcement branch:** send an application with documentary proof, your full address and the agency's RL number and address to sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd (02-41030255); action starts within 3 to 5 working days and the complaint is meant to be settled within 30 <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.
4. <a id="done-to-workers-item-4"></a>**If the agency sent you without permission,** write to the Ministry's Secretary with your smart card, a copy of the ticket, the contract and a copy of your passport; it is free and settled within 60 working days after BMET's recommendation reaches the Ministry <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.
5. <a id="done-to-workers-item-5"></a>**From Qatar,** email the Ministry's Complaint Resolution Cell, minister.expat@gmail.com <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. **Coming home after fraud,** complain at the airport welfare desk <sup>[136](#o136)</sup>.
6. <a id="done-to-workers-item-6"></a>**Mobile courts** (on-the-spot courts under the 2013 Act) give immediate remedies to people about to go and returned migrants, free, within 7 working days <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.
7. <a id="done-to-workers-item-7"></a>**If it isn't settled,** go up BMET's ladder: the Director (Employment), diremi@bmet.gov.bd, 01711111544 (3 months); then the Ministry's Secretary, secretary@probashi.gov.bd (1 month); then the Cabinet Division's grievance system at www.grs.gov.bd (3 months) <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>. The Ministry's own grievance officer is the Joint Secretary (Admin and Finance), 02-41030224, adsadmin@probashi.gov.bd (30 working days) <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.

**Unregistered broker or sub-agent**
A broker, often a friend or relative, collects workers and money for an agency, though since 2023 only a sub-agent registered and appointed by a licensed agency may do so <sup>[N16 (2024)](#n16), [33 (2023)](#o33)</sup>. Protection: [Step 1, item 3: Check the agency's licence](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-3) <sup>[33 (2023)](#o33)</sup>.

**Agency with a cancelled licence**
The agency's licence on OEP reads "Cancelled" although its validity date is years ahead (RL0345: valid to 31 December 2028) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>. Protection: [Step 1, item 3: Check the agency's licence](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-3) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.

**Fake job adverts and fake papers**
A job advert published without government or BMET approval, which is an offence in Bangladesh <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>; in 2019 a Dhaka gang posted fake Qatar job adverts, took Tk 3 to 4 lakh each from about 1,000 people in stages and showed fake offer letters, contracts and even a fake Qatar visa <sup>[N42 (2019)](#n42)</sup>. Protection: check the demand letter, permission letter and employer as in [Step 1, item 4: Check the job, the demand letter and the employer](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-4), and the visa as in [Step 5: Check the visa yourself](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself) <sup>[31 (2021)](#o31), [85 (2022)](#o85), [17](#o17)</sup>.

**Fake QVC agents, paid appointments and "medical fixers."** Someone claiming to be from QVC offers a job, charges for an appointment, asks for your VRN or passport number by phone, SMS or WhatsApp, promises to fix a medical result, or sells the paid lounge as a way to get the visa <sup>[35](#o35), [N30](#n30)</sup>. Protection: [Step 4, item 1: Book the appointment](#step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-1) (book only on qatarvisacenter.com; see [Fake QVC agents](#fake-qvc-agents)) <sup>[35](#o35), [N4 (2018)](#n4), [N30](#n30)</sup>.

**Visit or Hayya visa "with a job."** A broker sells a Hayya or tourist visa as a work visa <sup>[N62 (2023)](#n62)</sup>. Protection: [Step 5, item 3: Check it is a work visa](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-3); for the tourist-visa scams, see [Tourist visa: Hayya A1](#tourist-visa-hayya-a1), Scams <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

**High fees**
A licensed recruiter in Qatar asks you for fees, or someone asks for cash without a receipt or outside a bank <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2), [17](#o17)</sup>. Protection: Who pays what (see [How to pay](#how-to-pay)) <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.

**Contract substitution**
The job or wage in Qatar differs from what you were promised at home <sup>[N39 (2022)](#n39)</sup>. Protection: the contract you sign at QVC ([Step 4, item 3: At the centre](#step-4-qatar-visa-center-contract-fingerprints-and-medical-item-3)), print your authenticated contract after arrival ([Step 7, item 4: Get your contract](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-4)), and complain to MOL if they differ <sup>[126](#o126), [4](#o4)</sup>.

**Fake government sites, links and calls**
A link to a "government" site whose address doesn't end in gov.qa ([Step 5: Check the visa yourself](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself)), or an email or message asking for your password or verification code; report suspicious contacts to MOL on 16505 <sup>[N97](#n97), [N93](#n93)</sup>. Protection: use the official addresses given in the steps, such as the MOL contract portal on Hukoomi (hukoomi.gov.qa) in [Step 7, item 4: Get your contract](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-4) <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>.

**Hundi offers**
Someone offers an attractive exchange rate outside the approved exchange companies and banks <sup>[N98 (2022)](#n98)</sup>. Protection: see Sending money home; the law in Qatar is covered separately (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[44](#o44), [N48 (2024)](#n48)</sup>.

**Moving you on to Europe or America**
People or firms in Qatar take large sums promising to move migrants to Europe or America; on 14 January 2026 the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha warned against them after victims complained, and urged great caution before paying anyone <sup>[N99](#n99)</sup>. Embassy contacts: see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 8 <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-workers"></a>

#### 3.9.2 Tried by workers

QVC records your fingerprints, iris and face before the visa <sup>[36](#o36)</sup>, a residence permit obtained with false information or documents is revoked <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>, and getting a visa or work permit by illegal means is a crime in Bangladesh (see [How a job reaches you](#how-a-job-reaches-you-and-who-takes-a-cut), and who takes a cut) <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>.

<a id="your-rights-at-work"></a>

### 3.10 Your rights at work

<a id="contract"></a>

#### 3.10.1 Contract

**Which law covers you**
Qatar's Labour Law (Law 14 of 2004, as amended) sets company workers' rights <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. It doesn't cover domestic workers such as house drivers, nannies, cooks and gardeners (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker)), nor casual, agricultural (except in farm establishments that make or market their products, and workers who permanently run or repair farm machinery), part-time or freelance workers unless the Cabinet extends it <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. It also excludes government employees, people working at sea, workers of government-founded petroleum and petrochemical companies, workers of any company founded by or with QatarEnergy (the company the law's earlier text calls Qatar Petroleum) or in which it takes part, and others whose employment is regulated by special laws <sup>[4](#o4), [2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Workers who do household-type work for an employer outside the home, such as on boats, farms and private estates, come on a separate visa (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker)) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

**Your contract**
It must be written, authenticated by MOL and made in three copies: one for you, one for the employer and one kept by MOL <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. It must state the employer, your details, the date, the work, the start date, the duration and the agreed wage <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. It is in Arabic; the employer may attach a translation, but the Arabic text wins if they differ <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Print your authenticated copy and collect a hard copy from the company as in [Step 7, item 4: Get your contract](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-4) <sup>[126](#o126), [51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>. Probation lasts at most 6 months, and only once with the same employer <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. During probation the employer may end the contract if you turn out unsuitable for the work, but must tell you at least one month before the end date <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>; your own notice during probation is under If you want to change employer (see Notice) <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

<a id="pay-and-deductions"></a>

#### 3.10.2 Pay and deductions

**Minimum wage**
The minimum basic wage is QR 1,000 a month; if the employer doesn't provide suitable housing or food, it must also pay at least QR 500 a month for housing and QR 300 for food <sup>[9 (2020)](#o9)</sup>. It has applied since March 2021 to all sectors, domestic workers included <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. Any agreement to pay less is void, whatever your skill or job, and the rate is reviewed at least once a year <sup>[86 (2020)](#o86)</sup>. Amnesty International says the QR 1,000 set in 2021 has not been raised with living costs and is not enough for migrant workers <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are typical wages by trade for Bangladeshis in Qatar, beyond the QR 1,000 to 2,100 a month for housemaids, babysitters, cooks, cleaners, welders and helpers that BMET's posted Qatar letters give ([Step 1, item 3: Check the agency's licence](#step-1-find-a-real-job-item-3)) <sup>[68 (2024)](#o68), [70 (2024)](#o70), [72](#o72), [73](#o73)</sup>?

**When and how you are paid**
Wages are paid in Qatari riyals into your account at a bank or other financial institution in Qatar through WPS, on the due dates in [Step 7, item 7: Get your wages into a bank account](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-7); paying any other way doesn't clear the employer <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2), [90](#o90)</sup>. An employer that breaks the WPS rule can have new work permits and all its MOL transactions stopped until it shows it has paid all late wages <sup>[138 (2015)](#o138)</sup>. WPS blocks an employer automatically when it detects a worker unpaid or paid below the minimum wage, but it can't detect pay below the wage in your contract <sup>[N7](#n7)</sup>.

**Deductions**
The employer may not charge interest on a loan to you or deduct more than 10% of your wage to repay it, and all deductions together may not exceed 50% of the wage <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. You can't be forced to buy food or goods from the employer or from particular shops <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Nothing may be charged or deducted for safety equipment <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup> or for housing, its furniture or maintenance <sup>[45 (2014)](#o45)</sup>. Recruitment fees are covered under Who pays what (see [Before paying: what the law allows](#before-paying-what-the-law-allows)) <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

<a id="hours-rest-and-leave"></a>

#### 3.10.3 Hours, rest and leave

**Hours and overtime**
You may work at most 8 hours a day and 48 a week; in Ramadan at most 6 a day and 36 a week; travel between home and work doesn't count <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Breaks for prayer, rest and food total 1 to 3 hours, so that no one works more than 5 hours in a row <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. With overtime, total work may not exceed 10 hours a day, except to prevent serious loss or an accident <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Overtime is paid at your basic wage plus at least 25%, and work between 9 pm and 3 am at the basic wage plus at least 50%, except for shift workers <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

**Rest day and holidays**
At least 24 hours' paid rest in a row each week, normally Friday <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. If you are made to work on it, you get another rest day and the normal rest-day pay, or the basic wage plus at least 150%; except for shift workers, no one may work more than two Fridays in a row <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Paid public holidays: 3 working days for Eid al-Fitr, 3 for Eid al-Adha, 1 for National Day and 3 days chosen by the employer <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2), [4](#o4)</sup>.

**Leave**
After one full year of service, annual leave is at least 3 weeks a year (under 5 years' service) or 4 weeks (5 years or more), with part years counted pro rata <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>; the employer pays the wage earned and the leave pay before you go <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. You can't give up annual leave, and leave not taken is paid in cash when the contract ends <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Sick leave starts after 3 months of service and needs a certificate from a doctor the employer approves; it is on full pay for up to 2 weeks, half pay for 4 more weeks, then unpaid, and the employer may end the contract after 12 weeks if a specialist says you can't return <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. A Muslim worker can take unpaid special leave of up to 20 days once during service for Hajj <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. The employer may not end the contract, or give notice of ending it, while you are on any leave <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

**Heat**
From 1 June to 15 September, work in the sun or in open workplaces without shade and ventilation is banned from 10 am to 3:30 pm <sup>[139 (2021)](#o139)</sup>; the ban also covers enclosed or shaded places without adequate ventilation <sup>[N100](#n100)</sup>. Work must stop at any workplace where the WBGT (wet-bulb globe temperature, a heat-stress index) is above 32.1 degrees <sup>[139 (2021)](#o139)</sup>. The employer must give free cool drinking water throughout work, shaded rest areas, light loose clothing and free yearly medical checks for conditions that raise heat-stress risk, and must post the daily working-hours schedule where you can see it <sup>[139 (2021)](#o139)</sup>. If you have good reason to believe heat stress threatens your safety or health, you may stop work and complain to MOL, telling your supervisors, and the employer may not dismiss you, deny your rights or discriminate against you for it <sup>[139 (2021)](#o139)</sup>. Report breaches on MOL's hotline 40488264 <sup>[N101](#n101)</sup>. Sites that break the heat rules are closed for three days <sup>[140](#o140)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the heat ban cover motorcycle and bicycle delivery, given that in 2025 it was reported to stop such delivery in the restricted hours, leaving only air-conditioned vehicles such as cars <sup>[N102](#n102)</sup>?

<a id="housing-food-and-health-care"></a>

#### 3.10.4 Housing, food and health care

**Housing**
Where the employer must provide housing, it must meet the conditions set by ministerial decision <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. In shared rooms each bed must have at least 6 square metres, and corridors, roofs and basements may not be used as rooms <sup>[45 (2014)](#o45)</sup>. Each worker gets a bed with mattress and covers, lockable storage for documents and belongings, and enough air conditioning <sup>[45 (2014)](#o45)</sup>, with at least one full bathroom for every six workers <sup>[45 (2014)](#o45)</sup>. No money may be deducted for housing <sup>[45 (2014)](#o45)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is there an official number for housing complaints, apart from MOL's general complaint channels (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 2) <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>?

**Health insurance and care**
Health insurance is compulsory under Law 22/2021 and the employer pays the premiums, as in [Step 7, item 8: Get a health card](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-8) <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>. The employer also gives you a health insurance card and may not charge you for care caused by its failure to keep the cover valid <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>. Health care providers must give an insured person emergency care without asking for payment until the danger is over <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>. Holders of temporary residence permits are among those exempt <sup>[141 (2023)](#o141)</sup>. Get the health card as in [Step 7, item 8: Get a health card](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-8) <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>; renew it at PHCC or HMC or online through Hukoomi, and replacing a lost card costs QR 100, or QR 200 if it had expired <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>. QRCS (Qatar Red Crescent Society) runs four Workers' Health Centers with the Ministry of Public Health, at Al Hemaila (new Industrial Area), Mesaimeer, Fereej Abdul Aziz and Zekreet, with more than 20 specialties including round-the-clock services <sup>[142](#o142), [N103 (2024)](#n103)</sup>. Qatar's emergency number is 999 <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do the Mesaimeer and Al Hemaila centres still give 24/7 urgent care with free medicines, as QRCS's chief medical officer said in 2024 <sup>[N103 (2024)](#n103)</sup>?
> - Do they still accept any ID in place of a valid health card, as he said <sup>[N103 (2024)](#n103)</sup>?

<a id="your-passport-1"></a>

#### 3.10.5 Your passport

The employer's duty to return it, and the fine, are in [Step 7, item 3: Keep your passport](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-3) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. You must show your passport or residence permit when the authorities ask, and report a lost or damaged one at once <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Employers keeping passports remains common and largely unpunished (see [What ties you to the employer](#what-ties-you-to-the-employer)) <sup>[N54](#n54), [N6](#n6)</sup>. MOI's Search and Follow-up Department takes free complaints against a sponsor for abuse (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 7) <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the official place to report a kept passport <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>?

<a id="end-of-service-pay"></a>

#### 3.10.6 End-of-service pay

After one full year or more, you are owed at least three weeks' wage for each year of service, with part years counted pro rata <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. It is calculated on your last basic wage, and the employer may deduct amounts you owe it <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

<a id="your-residence-permit"></a>

### 3.11 Your residence permit

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-and-renewing"></a>

#### 3.11.1 How long you can stay and renewing

Your right to live in Qatar is your RP, which the employer issues and renews as in [Step 7, item 2: Make sure the employer issues your residence permit (RP) and QID](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-2) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Under the residence regulations (MOI Decision 25 of 2019) an RP is granted for one to five years, and the Minister may approve a longer or shorter period <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The fees ([Step 7, item 2: Make sure the employer issues your residence permit (RP) and QID](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-2)) are charged per year, with 20% off if the RP is renewed for 3 years <sup>[122](#o122), [144](#o144)</sup>. A fixed-term contract lasts at most 5 years and can be renewed by agreement; if both sides carry on after it ends, it becomes open-ended on the same terms <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

**Renewal**
The deadline and the late fine are in [Step 7, item 2: Make sure the employer issues your residence permit (RP) and QID](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-2) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [49 (2015)](#o49)</sup>. Renewal is done at MOI's Expatriates Affairs Department, MOI service centres, Metrash or MOI e-services, with the sponsor's signed form, the fees, your original passport and the company's establishment ID <sup>[144](#o144)</sup>. Check your QID or RP expiry date yourself, free, as in [Step 7, item 2: Make sure the employer issues your residence permit (RP) and QID](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-2) <sup>[123 (2021)](#o123)</sup>.

**Leaving Qatar during the contract (no exit permit)**
Qatar has abolished the exit permit for residents <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>. A worker under the Labour Law may leave temporarily or for good while the contract runs, unless the employer has asked in advance that named workers, at most 5% of its staff, need prior approval <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [11](#o11)</sup>. If you are stopped from leaving, complain to the Expatriate Exit Grievances Committee, which must decide within three working days <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. You may stay outside Qatar at most 6 months in a row without a return visa (see [Going home on leave](#going-home-on-leave)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

**When the RP ends**
How long you then have to leave Qatar is covered separately ([Leaving at the end of the job, item 5: Leave in time](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-5)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

**Permanent residence**
It needs 20 years in Qatar on a legal residence permit (10 if born in Qatar), enough income for the family, good conduct and enough Arabic <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>, and no more than 100 permits are granted a year unless the Amir approves more <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-on-leave"></a>

#### 3.11.2 Going home on leave

You can leave Qatar on leave without your employer's permission unless it has listed you in advance as needing prior approval (see Leaving Qatar during the contract (no exit permit)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [11](#o11)</sup>; your leave entitlement and the pay due before you go are under Your rights at work (see Leave) <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. MOI has a free Leave Notification service for expatriates leaving on leave, on MOI e-services, Metrash or at MOI service centres <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Must a company worker use MOI's Leave Notification service <sup>[147](#o147)</sup>?

**The ticket**
The Labour Law makes the employer pay the ticket at the end of service <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who pays a company worker's ticket for annual leave <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>?

**Coming back**
Absence without good reason for more than 7 days in a row after your leave lets the employer dismiss you without notice or gratuity <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. If you stay outside Qatar more than 6 months in a row, you need permission to return (a return visa), got before leaving or within a year of leaving by paying the fee, and a worker may not stay outside more than one year; on return the RP must be valid or expired no more than 60 days <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where do you apply for the return visa, given that a guide says it can be started in Metrash <sup>[N91](#n91)</sup>?
> - What is the return visa's fee?

**A loan to go back**
PKB's migration loan can also be taken for a re-entry visa, with extra papers listed in [Step 6, item 5: If you need a loan](#step-6-orientation-bmet-clearance-insurance-a-loan-and-the-flight-item-5) <sup>[43](#o43)</sup>.

**Passport or RP lost abroad**
A resident with a valid RP who lost or damaged the passport or RP outside Qatar can come back on a return visa <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The sponsor (or someone for him) fills in the form; you need a new valid passport if the passport was lost, and an attested certificate of the loss or damage (for the passport only) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where does the sponsor apply for this return visa <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>?
> - What is the fee <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>?
> - How long does it take <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>?

<a id="bringing-your-family"></a>

#### 3.11.3 Bringing your family

To have your family live with you, a private-sector worker needs a technical or specialised occupation and a salary of at least QR 10,000, or QR 6,000 with family housing, shown in the contract (see [Family residence](#family-residence-spouse-children-and-parents)) <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>. To host a family visit, you need a profession outside the labour category, a salary of at least QR 5,000 and verified family housing (see [Family visit visa](#family-visit-visa)) <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148), [78](#o78)</sup>.

<a id="what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported"></a>

### 3.12 What can get you jailed or deported

<a id="leaving-your-employer-overstaying-visa-trading"></a>

#### 3.12.1 Leaving your employer, overstaying, visa trading

A migrant licensed to work for one employer must not leave that employer's work or work for anyone he isn't authorised to work for <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. The exceptions: MOL may approve a secondment to another employer for up to six months, renewable once for the same period, or part-time work for another employer on top of your own job, in both cases with your employer's and your consent <sup>[10 (2020)](#o10), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Under the residence law (Law 21 of 2015), working against this rule, entering or leaving illegally, trading visas, overstaying or not leaving after your permit ends is punishable by up to three years in prison and/or a fine of up to QR 50,000 <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. MOI can instead offer a settlement for a fixed amount, and you may not leave Qatar until you pay it, are acquitted or serve the sentence, unless a guarantor undertakes to pay <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. The settlement is QR 12,000 for leaving the sponsor's work or working for another, trading visas, and not leaving after the RP ends <sup>[49 (2015)](#o49)</sup>. If you don't pay, the Minister can cancel your RP and deport you; he may also waive or reduce the amount for public-interest or humanitarian reasons <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Selling, transferring or trading visas, with or without payment, is banned <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>; a repeat offence within a year of finishing the sentence carries 30 days to 3 years in prison and/or a fine of QR 20,000 to QR 100,000 <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. The "free visa" is explained in Background (see [How a job reaches you](#how-a-job-reaches-you-and-who-takes-a-cut), and who takes a cut; see [What ties you to the employer](#what-ties-you-to-the-employer)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.

<a id="late-papers-and-absence"></a>

#### 3.12.2 Late papers and absence

Late RP steps are fined as in [Step 7, item 2: Make sure the employer issues your residence permit (RP) and QID](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-2), and overstaying a visit visa costs QR 200 a day up to QR 12,000 <sup>[49 (2015)](#o49)</sup>. Not reporting a lost passport or permit, or not showing them when asked, is fined up to QR 10,000 <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Absence from work is covered under If your employer reports you absent (see [If your employer reports you absent](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding), What it can lead to) <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. A residence permit obtained with false information or documents is revoked <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="strikes-and-protests"></a>

#### 3.12.3 Strikes and protests

A peaceful strike is allowed only if the employer has broken the contract or the law and an amicable settlement, conciliation or arbitration has failed, and only if <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>:
- three-quarters of the general committee of the workers of the profession or industry approve;
- written notice goes to the employer and MOL at least two weeks ahead;
- MOL approves in coordination with MOI;
- the strike stays at the workplace or residence;
- it lasts at most six working days;
- it disrupts no facilities and isn't in a vital facility.

A strike stops once the collective-dispute procedure starts, and strike days are unpaid leave <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. Inciting other workers to strike for unlawful reasons in a way that disrupts the business is a ground for dismissal without notice or gratuity <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. After unpaid Al Bandary workers protested in Doha in August 2022, police arrested protesters and workers from Bangladesh and four other countries were deported (see [What goes wrong most](#what-goes-wrong-most-and-what-people-wrongly-believe), and what people wrongly believe) <sup>[N64 (2022)](#n64)</sup>. Many workers still protest or strike over late wages despite the risk of arrest and deportation <sup>[N54](#n54)</sup>.

<a id="hundi-informal-money-transfer"></a>

#### 3.12.4 Hundi (informal money transfer)

MOI urges everyone to exchange money only through approved exchange companies and banks, and in 2024 its CID (Criminal Investigation Department) arrested a person for running hundi <sup>[N98 (2022)](#n98), [N48 (2024)](#n48)</sup>. Send money only through legal channels, never through hundi (see Sending money home) <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>.

<a id="drugs"></a>

#### 3.12.5 Drugs

- Importing or exporting drugs for trafficking: death or life imprisonment and QR 300,000 to 500,000 <sup>[149 (1987)](#o149)</sup>.
- Possessing, handing over or transporting drugs for trafficking: 10 to 20 years and QR 200,000 to 400,000 <sup>[149 (1987)](#o149)</sup>.
- Possessing, handing over or transporting them even without intent to trade or use them: 3 to 7 years and QR 100,000 to 200,000 <sup>[149 (1987)](#o149)</sup>.
- Possessing or using for personal use: 6 months to 3 years and QR 10,000 to 20,000 <sup>[149 (1987)](#o149)</sup>.

<a id="alcohol"></a>

#### 3.12.6 Alcohol

- Drinking in a public place or being drunk in the street: up to six months in prison and/or up to QR 3,000 <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Importing or making alcohol: up to three years and up to QR 10,000 <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Selling, buying, transporting or holding it for trade: up to three years and/or up to QR 10,000 <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Being clearly drunk or drugged during working hours: a ground for dismissal without notice or gratuity <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

<a id="cheques-and-debts"></a>

#### 3.12.7 Cheques and debts

Qatar's Penal Code (Law 11 of 2004) punishes giving in bad faith a cheque with no or not enough funds, withdrawing the funds, stopping payment, or signing it so it can't be cashed, with 3 months to 3 years in prison and/or a fine of QR 3,000-10,000, and the court may order the cheque amount paid (Art. 357) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>. The person owed can ask the prosecution or court to record a settlement with you, which ends the case, or stops the sentence if it comes later (Art. 359) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>. If the account holds less than the cheque amount, the bank must pay the part available unless the holder refuses, and give the holder a certificate to claim the rest (Law 1 of 2024) <sup>[151 (2024)](#o151)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can an ordinary unpaid debt (not a cheque) lead to prison or a travel ban <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>?

<a id="public-behaviour"></a>

#### 3.12.8 Public behaviour

- An obscene gesture, obscene songs or words, or an indecent act in or visible from a public place: up to 6 months and/or up to QR 3,000 (Art. 290) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Words, sounds, gestures or showing anything meant to offend a woman's modesty, or intruding on a woman's privacy: up to 1 year and/or up to QR 5,000 (Art. 291) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Harassing or disturbing others, or using immoral words, by phone, electronic media or any other means: up to 6 months and/or up to QR 3,000 (Art. 293) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Making, importing, holding or carrying for distribution or display, selling or sharing indecent pictures, films or other material: up to 1 year and/or up to QR 5,000 (Art. 292) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Gambling: up to 3 months and/or up to QR 3,000, or up to 6 months and/or QR 6,000 in a public place (Art. 275) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>.
- Insults and defamation (Arts. 326, 329, 330) <sup>[150 (2004)](#o150)</sup>:
  - publicly insulting someone: up to 1 year and/or up to QR 5,000;
  - public defamation: up to 2 years and/or QR 10,000;
  - an insult not in public, by phone or private message: up to 3 months and/or QR 1,000.

<a id="photos-recordings-and-online"></a>

#### 3.12.9 Photos, recordings and online

Under the Penal Code:
- Without consent, opening private letters, eavesdropping on calls, recording conversations in a private place, or photographing or filming people in a private place: up to 2 years and/or up to QR 10,000 (Art. 333) <sup>[152 (2017)](#o152)</sup>.
- Photographing or filming people in a public place to insult or defame them, or injured or dead people at accidents where the law doesn't allow it: the same penalty <sup>[152 (2017)](#o152)</sup>.
- Sharing or using such a recording or picture, even privately, without consent: up to 3 years; threatening to reveal it to force someone to act: up to 5 years; devices are confiscated and recordings destroyed (Art. 333 bis) <sup>[153 (2024)](#o153)</sup>.

Under the Cybercrime Law (Law 14 of 2014):
- Posting or sharing online pictures or videos of people in public places without their knowledge or consent: up to 1 year and/or up to QR 100,000 (Art. 8 bis) <sup>[154](#o154)</sup>.
- Online, attacking social principles or values, publishing news, pictures, audio or video about people's private or family life (even if true), or insulting or defaming others: up to 3 years and/or up to QR 100,000 (Art. 8) <sup>[155 (2014)](#o155)</sup>.
