- Spreading false news that endangers the state's safety or public order: up to 1 year and/or QR 250,000; running a website to spread it: up to 3 years and/or QR 500,000 (Art. 6) <sup>[155 (2014)](#o155)</sup>.
- Online threats or blackmail: up to 3 years and/or up to QR 100,000 (Art. 9) <sup>[155 (2014)](#o155)</sup>.
- Making or sharing child sexual material by IT means: up to 5 years and up to QR 500,000; possessing it: up to 1 year and/or up to QR 250,000; a child is anyone under 18 (Art. 7) <sup>[155 (2014)](#o155)</sup>.
- Forging an official electronic document or knowingly using one: up to 10 years and up to QR 200,000; online impersonation or fraud: up to 3 years and/or up to QR 100,000 (Arts. 10-11) <sup>[155 (2014)](#o155)</sup>.

<a id="violence-accidents-and-illegal-entry"></a>

#### 3.12.10 Violence, accidents and illegal entry

- Deliberately assaulting someone: up to 1 year and/or up to QR 25,000; an assault causing illness or inability to work for more than 20 days: up to 2 years and/or up to QR 50,000 (Arts. 308-309) <sup>[153 (2024)](#o153)</sup>.
- Causing a death by negligence or by not following laws or regulations: up to 3 years and/or up to QR 50,000, or up to 1 year and/or QR 20,000 if the victim's family forgives or accepts blood money (diya) (Art. 311) <sup>[153 (2024)](#o153)</sup>.
- Entering or leaving Qatar illegally, or helping anyone do so: up to 3 years and/or up to QR 100,000, doubled if the person is accused or sentenced, wanted, or under a travel or entry ban (Art. 139 bis) <sup>[153 (2024)](#o153)</sup>.

<a id="bans-on-coming-back"></a>

#### 3.12.11 Bans on coming back

A worker dismissed for disciplinary reasons who doesn't appeal, or loses, may not return to work in Qatar for four years <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. A worker who leaves without following the notice rules may not get a work permit for one year from departure <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. Someone deported by a court judgment may return only by decision of the Minister <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. The ban after an absconding case is under If your employer reports you absent (see [If your employer reports you absent](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding), What it can lead to) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong"></a>

### 3.13 If something goes wrong

<a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken"></a>

#### 3.13.1 If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken

Your wage is late if it isn't in your Qatar bank account by the dates in [Step 7, item 7: Get your wages into a bank account](#step-7-after-you-arrive-item-7) <sup>[90](#o90)</sup>. Workers at labour-supply companies are often told their wages are delayed until the client company pays <sup>[N26](#n26)</sup>. Since Law 9 of 2026, any dispute under the Labour Law or your contract must first go to MOL's department for amicable settlement, and only then to a Labour Dispute Settlement Committee <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. In January 2024 an MOL Assistant Undersecretary told the Bangladesh ambassador that an unpaid worker should complain or tell the Embassy within one month, because 3 to 4 months' unpaid wages become hard to recover, and that a worker unpaid for more than 2 to 3 months should look for work elsewhere <sup>[N104 (2024)](#n104)</sup>. Claims under the Labour Law or the contract lapse one year after the contract ends, and workers' lawsuits are free of court fees <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does MOL charge anything for a complaint <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>?

1. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-1"></a>**Keep your proof.** MOL's Labour Complaint Form asks for a copy of your employment contract (in Arabic) and your ID card <sup>[156](#o156)</sup>. An inspector may also ask for payslips, contracts or bank statements; bring copies of your passport and QID and the company's full name, location and the manager's or mandoob's (PRO's) contacts <sup>[N85](#n85)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-2"></a>**Complain to MOL** in any of these ways:
   - **Hotline:** MOL's 24/7 hotline answers in Arabic and English with translators for other languages <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>.

     > **Need to know**
     > - Which hotline number is current: 16008 <sup>[157](#o157)</sup> or 16505 / 44068979 <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>?

   - **Kiosk:** one of the 11 electronic complaint kiosks at labour ministry branches, working in 11 languages including Bengali <sup>[11](#o11), [N85](#n85)</sup>.
   - **SMS:** send "5" followed by your QID or visa number to 92727 <sup>[N105 (2020)](#n105), [N85](#n85)</sup>.
   - **Online:** MOL's Unified Platform for Complaints and Whistleblowers <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>:
     1. Log in with your QID or visa number and mobile number, or through NAS (the National Authentication System, Qatar's government login), and enter the one-time code <sup>[N85](#n85), [N106 (2021)](#n106)</sup>.
     2. Fill in the employer's details, the type of violation and your documents; the form asks whether you agree to referral to the committee if MOL can't settle it <sup>[N85](#n85), [156](#o156)</sup>.
     3. You get a complaint number and updates by SMS and email; check its status with the establishment registration number, complaint number and year <sup>[N85](#n85), [N107](#n107)</sup>. Workers report statuses such as "Notice sent" and "Sent to committee" <sup>[N107](#n107)</sup>.

     Anyone can also file a "public report" of a labour-law violation on the platform with only a mobile number; inspectors check it, and the sender isn't told the outcome <sup>[N106 (2021)](#n106)</sup>.

     > **Need to know**
     > - What is the web address of MOL's online complaint platform <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>?

3. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-3"></a>**What MOL must do.** It tries to settle the dispute within 7 days and presents the result within the next 7 days; an agreed settlement is written down, signed and enforceable like a court order <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. If there is no settlement, or a side rejects it or doesn't answer, MOL must refer the dispute within 3 working days to the Labour Dispute Settlement Committee <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. Filing the request stops the legal time limit on your claim while these steps run <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-4"></a>**The Labour Dispute Settlement Committee.** Each is chaired by a Court of First Instance judge, with an MOL representative and an accounting expert <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>. The hearing is set within 7 working days of referral, and both sides are told at least 3 days before <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. You may appear in person or authorise any representative, who need not be a relative <sup>[158](#o158)</sup>. Any document not in Arabic needs a certified Arabic translation from a licensed translation office <sup>[159 (2018)](#o159)</sup>. If you brought the case and neither you nor your representative attend any hearing, the committee may strike it out once it has confirmed you were properly told about the first hearing <sup>[159 (2018)](#o159)</sup>. The committee must decide within three weeks of the first hearing, and its decisions are enforceable at once <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2), [4](#o4)</sup>. Either side can appeal to the Court of Appeal within 15 days, counted from the decision if you were present or from the day after you were notified if you were absent; the appeal doesn't stop enforcement unless the court orders it, and the court decides within 30 days of its first hearing <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. In 2024 committee cases often took much longer than the three-week limit, some cases took several months, up to a year; workers with a verdict often still weren't paid because there is no time limit for enforcement, and court papers are all in Arabic <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>. Workers who can't find new jobs while their case is pending often leave Qatar without resolving it <sup>[N63](#n63)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-5"></a>**If the employer still doesn't pay: the Workers' Support and Insurance Fund (WSIF).** It pays workers the dues decided by the committees and recovers the money from the employer <sup>[53 (2018)](#o53)</sup>; it also pays on final court judgments, late monthly wages where the public interest requires, and emergencies, and it is meant to make sure workers get their dues if the employer goes out of business <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160), [11](#o11)</sup>. It pays at most three months' gross salary up to QR 20,000 for workers of operating companies, two months up to QR 12,000 for dissolved companies, and three months up to QR 8,000 for domestic workers, unless the Fund's chairman decides otherwise <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>. Apply, yourself or through a representative, on the Fund's form or electronic platform, with a certified copy of the committee decision bearing the enforcement formula; a representative needs a notarised power of attorney, and heirs of a dead worker can apply <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>. It pays only if the decision wasn't appealed within 15 days and you and the employer haven't made an amicable settlement; a written settlement shelves your application even if the employer never paid it <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>. It may pay earlier if you have left or are leaving Qatar for good <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>. Neither the committees nor the Fund cover your transport and document costs <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the Fund's platform address <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>?
   > - Where is the Fund's office <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>?
   > - How long does the Fund take to process an application <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>?

6. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-6"></a>**Your rights while unpaid.** If the employer breaks the wage-payment rule, you may ask MOL to change employer or end the contract, and the employer must then pay your journey home <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. You may also end the contract without notice and keep your full gratuity if the employer breaches the contract or the law <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. An employer that breaks the wage-payment rule faces up to one year in prison and/or a fine of QR 2,000 to 10,000 <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. If the Minister suspends the employer's dealings with MOL, this can't stop your contract attestation or the renewal of your work and residence permits <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>.
7. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-7"></a>**Abuse, danger and other routes in Qatar.** MOI's Search and Follow-up Department takes free complaints against a sponsor for abuse; bring a passport or ID copy and a written complaint <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>. The NHRC (National Human Rights Committee, set up under Law 38 of 2002 to protect workers' rights) <sup>[11](#o11)</sup> takes complaints by email c@nhrc.org.qa, phone 44048844, 66626663 or 33296666, fax 44444013, in person with its printed form, or online with a login; its hotline is 8002222 <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>. Write the complaint clearly, give your phone, email and address, and attach a copy of your passport or visa and any documents <sup>[161](#o161)</sup>. In danger, call 999 <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>; for human trafficking, the hotline is 16044 <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>.
8. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-8"></a>**The Bangladesh Embassy's labour wing.** Counsellor (Labour) +974 4037 4725; First Secretary (Labour) +974 4467 1499, labour.doha@mofa.gov.bd <sup>[92](#o92)</sup>. The Embassy's phone is +974 4467 1927 and its hotline +974 3366 2000 <sup>[137](#o137)</sup>. A worker in a labour dispute can get the Embassy's advice and help <sup>[162 (2021)](#o162)</sup>.
9. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-9"></a>**The Ministry in Dhaka.** Send its Welfare-1 branch an application with what happened, proof of status (passport, work permit, BMET smart card, Qatar ID number), the employer's name and address and the amounts owed <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>. The officer in charge is the Senior Assistant Secretary (Welfare-1): 02-41030251, mobile 01533944186, saswelfare@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>. It is free, and within 7 working days of receiving it the Ministry writes to the labour wing or the Welfare Board and tells you by letter <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>. If it doesn't act, the officer to report to is the Joint Secretary heading the Mission and Welfare wing: 02-41030219, mobile 01711427769, adsmission@probashi.gov.bd <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>. The Ministry's Complaint Resolution Cell also takes complaints from Bangladeshis abroad at minister.expat@gmail.com <sup>[135](#o135)</sup>. For legal help, apply to the Welfare Board (see [Legal help](#legal-help)) <sup>[163](#o163)</sup>.
10. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken-item-10"></a>**If you must leave before the case ends.** Any representative can act for you before the committee, and a representative applying to the Fund needs a notarised power of attorney <sup>[158](#o158), [160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>. In a 2026 case of unpaid Nepali workers, their embassy advised giving a power of attorney to legal representatives to continue the case, or changing employer <sup>[N108](#n108)</sup>.

    > **Need to know**
    > - Can you be deported before your complaint is settled, given that in January 2024 MOL said its link with MOI prevents this, but Bangladeshis had been sent home with cases pending, and authorities sometimes deported workers before their cases were resolved <sup>[N104 (2024)](#n104), [N6](#n6)</sup>?

<a id="if-you-are-injured-or-fall-ill"></a>

#### 3.13.2 If you are injured or fall ill

Treatment, pay during treatment and compensation for a work injury are under If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job, If you are injured or fall ill <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

<a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding"></a>

#### 3.13.3 If your employer reports you absent ("absconding")

**What it is**
An absconding report (in Arabic balagh huroob, a "runaway" report) is filed by an employer saying a worker has left the job <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>. The employer files it free, with a signed form, your passport or a copy and its ID or establishment ID, at MOI's Search and Follow-up Department, MOI service centres or Metrash, and must notify the authorities within 14 days of a worker leaving his job <sup>[48](#o48), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

**What it can lead to**
Leaving your employer's work is an offence under the residence law, settled at QR 12,000 or prosecuted, with deportation if the settlement isn't paid (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [49 (2015)](#o49)</sup>. Absence without good reason for more than 7 days in a row, or 15 days in total in a year, also lets the employer dismiss you without notice or gratuity <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. The bans on coming back under the residence law are covered separately (see [Bans on coming back](#bans-on-coming-back)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the Embassy's 2021 guidance still apply: that if you wait more than two months after learning of the case, or really have run away, the Public Prosecution or a court can send you home with a QR 12,000 fine and, if it is unpaid, a ban of up to 5 years, while a case under one month old lets you come back without a fine <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>?

1. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-1"></a>**If you have a labour dispute, complain to MOL quickly,** as in If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken, item 2 <sup>[4](#o4), [164 (2016)](#o164)</sup>. In practice workers still have to dispute a charge in person while employers file online <sup>[N6](#n6), [N55 (2023)](#n55)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Can an employer still file an absconding report after a worker has complained to MOL, as the government said in 2016 it could not <sup>[164 (2016)](#o164)</sup>?

2. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-2"></a>**Contest it at once.** Go to the Niyabah (prosecution) court inside the CEID building, ask for the form for a grievance against the absconding report, fill it in typed, attach your ID, timesheet and agreement, and attend the hearing date you are given <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-3"></a>**If you are already working elsewhere,** you can surrender with a valid passport at the "Musallami Anfusahum" surrender section of the Search and Follow-up Department and apply to go home; the officer tells you when you may return; without a valid passport (see [Going home without valid papers](#going-home-without-valid-papers)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-item-4"></a>**Ask the Embassy's labour wing** for advice and help; its contacts are in If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken, item 8 <sup>[162 (2021)](#o162), [92](#o92)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a worker check whether a report has been filed (for example in Metrash) <sup>[48](#o48), [130](#o130)</sup>?

**In practice**
Employers have misused absconding reports to block job changes, used MOL's "Leave Country Notice" option to cancel workers' QIDs (which the worker could lift only in person at a labour office), and avoided paying gratuity by claiming the worker had absconded; leaving to escape abuse still counts as absconding (see [What ties you to the employer](#what-ties-you-to-the-employer)) <sup>[N55 (2023)](#n55), [N54](#n54)</sup>.

<a id="legal-help"></a>

#### 3.13.4 Legal help

If you or your family need legal help in Qatar, apply to the Welfare Board directly, or through the expatriate welfare branch of the DC's (district administrator's) office or the DEMO (District Employment and Manpower Office, BMET's office in the district); the Board writes to the Embassy asking it to act and tells you what it has done <sup>[163](#o163)</sup>.

<a id="changing-employer-or-leaving"></a>

### 3.14 Changing employer or leaving

<a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer"></a>

#### 3.14.1 If you want to change employer

**No permission needed**
Since 2020 you can move to another employer without your current employer's consent: the NOC requirement was abolished <sup>[10 (2020)](#o10), [11](#o11)</sup>. Under MOI Decision 51/2020 you change employer by notifying MOL under its rules, with your RP valid or within 90 days of its expiry, unless it expired for reasons beyond your control <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>. The old limits (the end of a fixed-term contract, or five years with the employer) were repealed <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Before your RP is issued, the authorities may move your work visa to another employer with MOL's approval <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

**Notice**
During probation, give the current employer one month's written notice; the new employer compensates it for the ticket and recruitment fees, up to two months' basic salary <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. After probation, notice is one month during your first two years of work and two months after that <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. If you give less, you pay the employer your basic wage for the part not given <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. The current employer can waive or shorten the notice on MOL's platform <sup>[N109](#n109)</sup>.

1. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-1"></a>**Check first:** your RP must be valid or expired less than 90 days ago <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is MOL's official eligibility list, beyond press reports that MOL requires no ban on you or the company, an active registration for the new employer, no other change request pending, no non-compete restriction, a valid QID and a local mobile number in your own name <sup>[N49](#n49), [N109](#n109)</sup>?
   > - Does an absconding report filed before your request still stop the change, as a labour ministry official said in 2020 <sup>[N51 (2020)](#n51)</sup>?

2. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-2"></a>**Apply:** log in to MOL's Individuals Portal through NAS (the new employer uses the Establishments Portal); either you or the new employer can file <sup>[N49](#n49), [N109](#n109)</sup>. Choose My Services, then Change of employer within the labour market, then Apply; choose the employer types, enter the new contract and submit; "Request Submitted Successfully" appears <sup>[N109](#n109)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-3"></a>**If the new employer files,** you get an SMS and have three days to approve it in My Requests, or it is rejected automatically <sup>[N109](#n109)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-want-to-change-employer-item-4"></a>**What follows:** the current employer is told and may comment, MOL decides, the new contract is authenticated, the notice period runs and MOI then updates your RP <sup>[N49](#n49)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is MOL's official change-employer guide <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>?
> - Is there any fee for changing employer <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>?

**If it is blocked**
Complain to MOL as in If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken, item 2 <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>; unpaid workers have a right to move ([If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 6) <sup>[4](#o4)</sup>. In practice, workers are still often asked for a signed resignation letter or an NOC from their employer, and some employers retaliated by cancelling the visa or filing an absconding report during the notice period <sup>[N54](#n54), [N50 (2023)](#n50), [N6](#n6)</sup>. Some workers were forced to leave Qatar and re-enter on a new visa <sup>[N55 (2023)](#n55)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can you still change employer inside Qatar once your RP is cancelled, given that an advisory firm reported in June 2026 that a local transfer is then not allowed and the person must be hired from abroad <sup>[N110](#n110)</sup>?

<a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job"></a>

#### 3.14.2 Leaving at the end of the job

When the contract ends, the employer must pay everything you are owed and send you home at its cost <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.

1. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-1"></a>**Collect what you are owed.** The employer must pay your wage and all dues by the end of the next working day, or within 7 days if you left without notice, including the gratuity and cash for annual leave not taken (see [Your rights at work](#your-rights-at-work)) <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. On request it must give you, free, a service certificate (dates, job, wage) and return the certificates and documents you deposited <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.
2. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-2"></a>**A signed settlement ends your Fund claim** (see [If you aren't paid](#if-you-arent-paid-are-abused-or-your-passport-is-taken), are abused or your passport is taken, item 5) <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>. Workers have been sent home early on "long leave" without end-of-service benefits, and one worker thumbprinted a paper he couldn't read saying he had received all dues while still owed most of his gratuity <sup>[N52 (2022)](#n52)</sup>. If dues are unpaid, complain as in If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken, item 2; the Fund may pay earlier to a worker leaving Qatar for good <sup>[4](#o4), [160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>.
3. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-3"></a>**Your ticket home.** The employer must send you back, at its cost, to the place it recruited you from and finish the return procedures within two weeks of the contract ending; if you take another job before leaving, the new employer takes over this duty <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.
4. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-4"></a>**Cancel the RP.** The cancellation form is signed by both the sponsor and you; it costs QR 20 (personal sponsorship) or QR 50 (company), at MOI service centres, Metrash or MOI e-services <sup>[165](#o165)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Must MOL approve the end of the contract before MOI cancels the RP, as the advisory firm Deloitte reported in 2025, adding that cancellation then takes 5 to 7 working days <sup>[N111](#n111)</sup>?

5. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-5"></a>**Leave in time.** You must leave Qatar within 90 days after your RP expires or is cancelled <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>; not leaving is an offence settled at QR 12,000 (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[49 (2015)](#o49)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does MOI now allow only 14 days after cancellation, given that an officer of MOI's Airport Passports Department was reported in June 2026 as saying people must leave within 14 days of cancellation and pay QR 10 a day after that, and an advisory firm says staying past 14 days may lead to a ban on future entry <sup>[N112](#n112), [N113](#n113), [N110](#n110)</sup>?

6. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-6"></a>**Clear fines and travel bans.** An unpaid MOI settlement stops you leaving unless a guarantor undertakes to pay (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Overstay fines, traffic fines and other dues can be paid in Metrash before you go to the airport, and anyone with a travel ban must follow up with the department that issued it to have it lifted <sup>[N112](#n112), [N113](#n113)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the official way to check for a travel ban <sup>[N114 (2022)](#n114), [N91](#n91)</sup>?
   > - What is the official way to lift a travel ban <sup>[N114 (2022)](#n114), [N91](#n91)</sup>?
   > - Do travel bans show in Metrash <sup>[N114 (2022)](#n114), [N91](#n91)</sup>?

7. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-7"></a>**Declare cash and gold.** Anyone leaving or entering Qatar with cash, bearer instruments such as cheques or traveller's cheques, precious metals such as gold, or precious stones worth QR 50,000 or more must declare the value to the Customs officer and fill in the Customs form; the same applies to sending them with another person, by shipping or by mail <sup>[166 (2019)](#o166)</sup>. If you don't, Customs seizes them, asks where they came from, may detain you and hand you to MOI, and refers the case to the Public Prosecution <sup>[166 (2019)](#o166)</sup>. Deliberately not declaring, declaring falsely or refusing to explain the source carries up to 3 years in prison, or a fine of QR 100,000-500,000 or double the value carried, whichever is more <sup>[167 (2019)](#o167)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Where do travellers get the Customs declaration form <sup>[166 (2019)](#o166)</sup>?

8. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-8"></a>**At the airport.** No exit permit is needed, and if you are stopped from leaving, complain as in Your residence permit (see Leaving Qatar during the contract (no exit permit)) <sup>[11](#o11), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. A worker who leaves without following the notice rules may not get a new work permit for one year from departure (see [Bans on coming back](#bans-on-coming-back)) <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>.
9. <a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-9"></a>**What you bring home.** Bangladesh's baggage, gold and currency limits are under Coming home (see [What you can bring home](#what-you-can-bring-home)) <sup>[168](#o168), [169 (2020)](#o169)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has the gratuity savings and investment system that Prime Minister's Decision 34 of 2025 set a committee to propose been approved <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>?
> - Does it cover private-sector migrant workers <sup>[170](#o170)</sup>?

**Employer tricks at the end of the job**
"Long leave", a paper saying all dues were paid, or a false absconding claim to avoid paying gratuity <sup>[N52 (2022)](#n52), [N55 (2023)](#n55)</sup>. Protection: the settlement warning ([Leaving at the end of the job, item 2: A signed settlement ends your Fund claim](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-2)) and the absconding rules (see [If your employer reports you absent](#if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding)) <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>.

<a id="work-visa-domestic-worker"></a>

## 4 Work visa: domestic worker

Domestic workers are housemaids, house drivers, nannies, cooks and gardeners working for a household under its direction <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. They come under Qatar's Domestic Workers Law (Law No. 15 of 2017), not the Labour Law <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15), [4](#o4)</sup>, but their recruitment, medical test and residence permit follow the rules for other migrant workers unless that law says otherwise <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. A separate visa, on the same conditions, covers household-linked work outside the employer's home, such as on fishing and pleasure boats, farms and private estates (izab) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Do workers on the boat, farm or estate visa come under the Domestic Workers Law or the Labour Law? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Does BMET clear Bangladeshis for boat, farm or estate jobs? <sup>[18](#o18)</sup>

**Is the route open from Bangladesh?** The route is open for women: BMET's posted Qatar permission letters include 100 women housemaids for OKAZ Manpower, Al Messila, through BTS Overseas (RL-2434), dated 5 August 2025, and the larger housemaid, babysitter and cook orders listed in Work visa: company worker ([Step 1, item 3](#step-1-check-you-qualify)) <sup>[32](#o32), [72](#o72), [73](#o73)</sup>. Bangladesh adds stricter age and training rules for women ([Step 1: Check you qualify](#step-1-check-you-qualify); [Step 2: Register on OEP and (women) take the housekeeping course](#step-2-register-on-oep-and-women-take-the-housekeeping-course)) <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has BMET approved newer Qatar domestic-worker orders since it stopped posting letters in December 2025? <sup>[18](#o18), [76](#o76)</sup>

<a id="who-pays-what-1"></a>

### 4.1 Who pays what

In Qatar a licensed recruitment office may not charge a recruited worker any fees, recruitment expenses or other costs <sup>[2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>, and the employer may not deduct any cost of bringing the worker to Qatar from the wage <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. The household pays the office: at most QR 14,000 for a worker from Bangladesh, the cap set in January 2022 by Qatar's Ministry of Commerce and Industry with MOL, none of it chargeable to the worker <sup>[41 (2022)](#o41), [2 (2004)](#o2)</sup>. In Bangladesh, an agency approved to send women domestic workers may take no migration cost from them ([Step 3, item 1: Women: go only through an approved agency](#step-3-choose-an-approved-agency-item-1)) <sup>[171](#o171), [91](#o91)</sup>. In the BTS Overseas letter the employer bears the migration cost and every cost up to arrival in Qatar, including the levy and airfare <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which levy does the BTS Overseas permission letter mean? <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>
> - What may an agency take from a man going as a house driver, cook or gardener? <sup>[171](#o171)</sup>

<a id="how-a-household-in-qatar-recruits"></a>

### 4.2 How a household in Qatar recruits

A household recruits through a licensed recruitment office, or directly from abroad with the labour department's approval <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. In September 2026 MOL launched the first phase of Moawen, its online platform for recruiting domestic workers, at moawen.mol.gov.qa <sup>[N115](#n115)</sup>, and published a list of 221 registered recruitment companies on its website, telling people to check an office is on it before paying <sup>[N33](#n33), [N116](#n116)</sup>. The employer applies for the visa with the form, a copy of the worker's valid passport, the employer's ID card, the certified work contract and the worker's police clearance (as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 2: Women: take the housekeeping course](#step-2-register-on-oep-and-women-take-the-housekeeping-course-item-2)) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [8](#o8)</sup>. On MOI's Group Visa service, an individual recruiting house workers also needs the approval of MOI's Committee Reviewing Recruitment Applications, with the application form, the worker's passport copy and the sponsor's ID copy <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>. The fee is QR 300 for a Qatari sponsor and QR 1,800 for an expatriate sponsor (QR 1,500 for the approval plus QR 300 for the visa) <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>, and none of it may be taken from the worker's wage <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are Moawen's steps and fees? <sup>[N115](#n115)</sup>
> - Can a worker see her case on Moawen? <sup>[N115](#n115)</sup>
> - Where is MOL's current list of licensed offices published? <sup>[N33](#n33)</sup>
> - Is the Group Visa fee per worker? <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>
> - How long does Group Visa approval take? <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>
> - Can a Group Visa refusal be appealed? <sup>[108](#o108)</sup>

<a id="step-1-check-you-qualify"></a>

### 4.3 Step 1: Check you qualify

1. <a id="step-1-check-you-qualify-item-1"></a>**Women: check the housekeeping-course rules.** You must be 25 to 45 when you enrol in the course (returnees up to 50), have passed class 5 (which can be relaxed in special cases) and be physically and mentally fit <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>. You cannot enrol if you are pregnant, if your youngest child is under 6, or if you have a contagious or incurable disease <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-check-you-qualify-item-2"></a>**Men going as house drivers, cooks or gardeners: check Qatar's age limit.** Qatar allows domestic workers of either sex aged 18 to 60 only, and the minister may waive the upper limit <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does Bangladesh set any age limit for men going to Qatar as house drivers? <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>
   > - Does Bangladesh set any test or licence rule for men going to Qatar as house drivers? <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>

<a id="step-2-register-on-oep-and-women-take-the-housekeeping-course"></a>

### 4.4 Step 2: Register on OEP and (women) take the housekeeping course

1. <a id="step-2-register-on-oep-and-women-take-the-housekeeping-course-item-1"></a>**Register on OEP** with your NID, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 1, item 1: Women: check the housekeeping-course rules](#step-1-check-you-qualify-item-1) <sup>[63](#o63), [44](#o44)</sup>.

2. <a id="step-2-register-on-oep-and-women-take-the-housekeeping-course-item-2"></a>**Women: take the housekeeping course.** It is compulsory for women going to the Middle East for domestic work: one month (24 working days), residential, at one of 41 BMET TTCs <sup>[17](#o17), [21](#o21)</sup>.

   You'll need <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>:
   - proof that you passed class 5
   - a no-objection letter from your legal guardian
   - a copy of your birth certificate, NID or passport

   1. BMET posts the admission notice online; apply through OEP's training portal (as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 1, item 2: Men going as house drivers, cooks or gardeners: check Qatar's age limit](#step-1-check-you-qualify-item-2)), choose the centre and date, and pay the set fee against a money receipt <sup>[21](#o21), [44](#o44), [17](#o17)</sup>.
   2. Show the originals at the interview and enrol in person <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.
   3. Attend 90% of the sessions and score 70% in the final 100-mark test <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>. The course includes how to contact the police and the Bangladesh embassy in the destination country <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.
   4. Download and print the certificate online <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>. It is valid 2 years for a first departure; if it is 2 to 3 years old you do a 3-day refresher, and if older, the full course again; returnees with at least a year abroad do the 3-day refresher <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What is the housekeeping course fee in taka? <sup>[21](#o21)</sup>
   > - Does the course replace the compulsory 3-day PDO, which BMET lists separately? <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>

<a id="step-3-choose-an-approved-agency"></a>

### 4.5 Step 3: Choose an approved agency

1. <a id="step-3-choose-an-approved-agency-item-1"></a>**Women: go only through an approved agency.** On 1 September 2025 the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare approved 21 licensed agencies to send women domestic workers abroad (to all destinations), each with a Tk 15 lakh security deposit with BMET and at least one woman on its office staff <sup>[171](#o171)</sup>. An approved agency may take no migration cost from you, and signs an undertaking that its licence may be cancelled if you are abused at work <sup>[171](#o171), [91](#o91)</sup>. If one asks you for money, complain to BMET as in Work visa: company worker, Scams, "Free visa" or a bought visa, item 2 <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Which agencies are on the current approved list for women domestic workers? <sup>[171](#o171)</sup>

2. <a id="step-3-choose-an-approved-agency-item-2"></a>**Check the agency's licence** on OEP's agency list by its RL number, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 1, item 3](#step-1-check-you-qualify) <sup>[66](#o66)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-3-choose-an-approved-agency-item-3"></a>**If a broker brings you the job:** an agency may appoint only a registered person as its sub-agent; see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 1, item 3](#step-1-check-you-qualify) <sup>[33 (2023)](#o33)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract"></a>

### 4.6 Step 4: Check the job, the employer and the contract

1. <a id="step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-1"></a>**Look for BMET's permission letter** for the job order, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 1, item 4](#step-1-check-you-qualify) <sup>[18](#o18)</sup>. The BTS Overseas letter gave <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>:
   - a 2-year contract
   - 3 months' probation
   - 8 hours a day and 48 a week
   - ticket, food, medical care and housing paid
   - a salary of 1,000 to 1,200 (currency not stated)
2. <a id="step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-2"></a>**Check the contract.** A domestic worker may be employed only under a written contract certified by MOL, in three copies: one for you, one for the employer and one filed with MOL <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. It must state <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>:
   - both sides' names, nationality and addresses
   - the type of work
   - the start date
   - probation, length, renewal and ending terms
   - the wage, and how and when it is paid

   It is in Arabic; a translation may be attached, but the Arabic wins if they differ <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. In the BTS Overseas letter BMET required the agency to give each worker an attested Bangla translation <sup>[32](#o32)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-3"></a>**Check the wage:** at least QR 1,000 a month basic, plus QR 500 for housing and QR 300 for food unless the employer provides them <sup>[172 (2020)](#o172), [11](#o11)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-4"></a>**Check the household's recruitment office is on MOL's list** ([How a household in Qatar recruits](#how-a-household-in-qatar-recruits)) <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15), [N33](#n33), [N116](#n116)</sup>.

<a id="step-5-the-employer-gets-the-visa-attested-at-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha"></a>

### 4.7 Step 5: The employer gets the visa attested at the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha

1. <a id="step-5-the-employer-gets-the-visa-attested-at-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha-item-1"></a>**The Qatari sponsor submits the visa through an agent enlisted with the Embassy,** with the sponsor-housemaid agreement, the visa, the insurance, your passport copy and the sponsor's Qatar ID and contacts <sup>[173 (2021)](#o173)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-the-employer-gets-the-visa-attested-at-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha-item-2"></a>**The fee is QR 50 per visa;** papers are taken 7:30 am to 2 pm, Sunday to Thursday, and returned after 3 working days <sup>[173 (2021)](#o173)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-the-employer-gets-the-visa-attested-at-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha-item-3"></a>**Without this attestation you can't travel:** Bangladesh does not clear a housemaid whose visa the Embassy has not attested <sup>[173 (2021)](#o173)</sup>.

<a id="step-6-check-the-visa-and-do-the-medical"></a>

### 4.8 Step 6: Check the visa and do the medical

1. <a id="step-6-check-the-visa-and-do-the-medical-item-1"></a>**Check the visa is real and its type** on MOI's Visa Inquiry, by visa number or passport number, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1: The Qatari sponsor submits the visa through an agent enlisted with the Embassy](#step-5-the-employer-gets-the-visa-attested-at-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha-item-1) <sup>[83](#o83), [112](#o112)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-6-check-the-visa-and-do-the-medical-item-2"></a>**The medical test** follows the rules for other migrant workers <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Do household visas go through QVC, where company workers give fingerprints, sign the contract and do the medical in Bangladesh ([Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 4: Check the job, the employer and the contract](#step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract))? <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15), [36](#o36)</sup>

<a id="step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan"></a>

### 4.9 Step 7: BMET clearance, the smart card and a loan

1. <a id="step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan-item-1"></a>**BMET clearance** (the smart card, your emigration clearance) works as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 6, item 3](#step-6-check-the-visa-and-do-the-medical); for a woman domestic worker the agency must also submit proof that she is 25 to 45 and her legal guardian's no-objection letter <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan-item-2"></a>**A loan if you need one.** PKB has a loan for women migrants of up to Tk 3 lakh at 9% simple interest over up to 3 years; apply as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 6, item 5](#step-6-check-the-visa-and-do-the-medical) <sup>[119](#o119)</sup>.

<a id="step-8-after-you-arrive"></a>

### 4.10 Step 8: After you arrive

1. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-item-1"></a>**Residence permit and QID.** Within 30 days of entry you do the medical check and fingerprints, and your sponsor gets your RP, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 7, item 2: A loan if you need one](#step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan-item-2) <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15), [14 (2019)](#o14), [37](#o37)</sup>. For a first RP on personal sponsorship MOI asks for the application form signed by the sponsor, your original passport, a photo and a blood group certificate; the fees are QR 100 a year for the work permit and QR 300 a year for the RP, plus QR 100 for the card <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>. An individual sponsor collects the card at MOI's Expatriates' Affairs Department or has it sent by Qatar Post <sup>[38 (2021)](#o38)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-item-2"></a>**Your contract.** Keep your copy <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>; you can also print the contract registered in Qatar, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 7, item 4](#step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan) <sup>[126](#o126)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-item-3"></a>**Pay** is due by the 3rd of the next month, into your bank account or in cash against a receipt you sign <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. At QIB, for example, the Domestic Workers Account is opened only in the QIB Mobile App <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>:
   1. Enter your QID, mobile number and the OTP <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.
   2. Give your employer's QID (the employer must bank with QIB) <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.
   3. Scan your QID and passport and take a selfie <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.

   There is no minimum balance and the debit card is free <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>. When the job ends the employer must cancel the card and close the account, and any balance comes back to you as a banker's cheque <sup>[174](#o174)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-item-4"></a>**Health card.** Get it as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 7, item 8](#step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan); for domestic staff PHCC also asks for your sponsor's valid QID, and the fee is QR 50 <sup>[50](#o50)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-8-after-you-arrive-item-5"></a>**SIM card.** A prepaid line needs your passport or a valid QID <sup>[127](#o127)</sup>; more in Work visa: company worker, [Step 7, item 5](#step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan).

<a id="scams-1"></a>

### 4.11 Scams

<a id="done-to-workers-1"></a>

#### 4.11.1 Done to workers

Check every office, website and visa yourself before you pay anything <sup>[N116](#n116), [83](#o83)</sup>.

**Fake Moawen and MOL sites and accounts**
On 4 November 2026 MOL warned against websites pretending to be Moawen; the real one is moawen.mol.gov.qa (hotline 16505, info@mol.gov.qa) <sup>[N117](#n117)</sup>. Qatari government websites end in gov.qa; use the official addresses given in the steps, such as the contract portal on hukoomi.gov.qa ([Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 7, item 4](#step-7-bmet-clearance-the-smart-card-and-a-loan)) <sup>[N93](#n93), [126](#o126)</sup>. Fake accounts claiming ties to MOL may try to collect personal information; MOL says never share your name, address, ID numbers, passwords or financial details <sup>[N118](#n118)</sup>.

**Unlicensed offices' social-media adverts**
Unlicensed offices post convincing adverts and pretend to be agencies; check the office is on MOL's list before paying <sup>[N116](#n116)</sup>.

**Fake domestic-worker recruitment offices**
Unlicensed offices post convincing social-media adverts for domestic-worker recruitment <sup>[N116](#n116)</sup>. Protection: [Step 4, item 4: Check the household's recruitment office is on MOL's list](#step-4-check-the-job-the-employer-and-the-contract-item-4) <sup>[N116](#n116), [N117](#n117)</sup>.

**Agencies charging women**
See [Step 3, item 1: Women: go only through an approved agency](#step-3-choose-an-approved-agency-item-1) <sup>[171](#o171), [17](#o17)</sup>.

**A visit visa sold as a job**
A Hayya visitor may not work in Qatar by law; check the visa type ([Step 6, item 1: Check the visa is real and its type](#step-6-check-the-visa-and-do-the-medical-item-1)) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="tried-by-workers-1"></a>

#### 4.11.2 Tried by workers

An RP obtained with false information or documents is revoked <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>, and getting a visa or work permit by illegal means is a crime in Bangladesh (see [How a job reaches you](#how-a-job-reaches-you-and-who-takes-a-cut), and who takes a cut) <sup>[28](#o28)</sup>. BMET clearance for a woman domestic worker needs proof of age 25 to 45 <sup>[17](#o17)</sup>.

<a id="your-rights-at-work-1"></a>

### 4.12 Your rights at work

<a id="care-and-dignity"></a>

#### 4.12.1 Care and dignity

The employer must give you free food, decent housing, health care and medicines for illness or work injury <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. It must not harm you physically or psychologically, never make you work on sick leave, and not make you work during your daily rest or weekly day off unless you both agree <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

<a id="hours-rest-and-leave-1"></a>

#### 4.12.2 Hours, rest and leave

You work at most 10 hours a day unless otherwise agreed, plus breaks for prayer, rest and meals that don't count as work <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. You get a paid weekly day off of at least 24 hours in a row, on the day in the contract <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>, and 3 weeks' paid annual leave a year <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. Probation is paid, its length set by ministerial decision <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

<a id="tickets-and-end-of-service-pay"></a>

#### 4.12.3 Tickets and end-of-service pay

The employer gives you a return ticket home every 2 years for leave and a one-way ticket at the end <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. After a full year, you are owed end-of-service pay of at least 3 weeks' wage per year of service, pro rata <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

<a id="who-you-work-for"></a>

#### 4.12.4 Who you work for

You may not work for anyone else <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>, but working for the employer's parents, children or spouse is allowed <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The employer may not make you work outside Qatar without your consent <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

<a id="injury-and-death"></a>

#### 4.12.5 Injury and death

A work injury is compensated as under the Labour Law <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. If a worker dies, the employer pays to send the body home if the family asks, and deposits all the worker's dues with the labour department within 15 days <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

<a id="penalties-and-practice"></a>

#### 4.12.6 Penalties and practice

An employer who breaks the contract, care, hours, rest or leave rules can be fined up to QR 5,000, and up to QR 10,000 for breaking the age or wage rules <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. Most of the 105 women domestic workers Amnesty International interviewed in 2020 regularly worked over 14 hours a day and had had their passports taken <sup>[N53 (2020)](#n53)</sup>, and domestic workers, mostly women, still faced verbal, physical and sexual abuse in 2025 <sup>[N59](#n59)</sup>.

<a id="bringing-your-family-1"></a>

### 4.13 Bringing your family

Family residence needs a technical or specialised job and a salary of QR 10,000, or QR 6,000 with family housing (see [Family residence](#family-residence-spouse-children-and-parents)) <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Who can sponsor the RP of a baby born in Qatar to a domestic worker without family residence? <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>

<a id="if-something-goes-wrong-1"></a>

### 4.14 If something goes wrong

<a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused"></a>

#### 4.14.1 If you aren't paid or are abused

Claims under the Domestic Workers Law or the contract expire 1 year after the contract ends or you leave <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>.

1. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused-item-1"></a>**Complain as in Work visa: company worker, If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken.** Domestic workers' disputes go to the same labour dispute settlement committees as other workers', with the same time limits and appeal <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. On MOL's Unified Platform for Complaints and Whistleblowers a domestic worker logs in with a QID or entry-visa number and mobile number to complain against the employer <sup>[N106 (2021)](#n106)</sup>. Abuse by the sponsor goes to MOI's Search and Follow-up Department, free, with a passport or ID copy and a written complaint <sup>[143](#o143)</sup>. In danger, call 999 <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.
2. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused-item-2"></a>**If a committee decision isn't paid,** WSIF pays the dues and recovers them from the employer <sup>[53 (2018)](#o53)</sup>; for a domestic worker it pays at most three months' gross salary, up to QR 8,000, unless its chairman decides otherwise <sup>[160 (2022)](#o160)</sup>.
3. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused-item-3"></a>**A recruitment office breaking MOL's rules** on the probation period or the maximum recruitment price: MOL gave the hotline 40288101 for reporting it in 2023 <sup>[175 (2023)](#o175)</sup>.
4. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused-item-4"></a>**Trafficking and shelter.** Call 16044 <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>. Victims can be sheltered at MOL's Humanitarian Care House, with housing, meals, basic health care, legal support and help to go home <sup>[157](#o157)</sup>; it has six villas for up to 200 people and is run by QRCS, stays are up to two weeks, longer if a court case is pending, staff must tell the police when they admit someone, and it has also housed workers with labour-abuse cases <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>. The government-funded Aman Center houses women who fled abusive employers, even if the employer filed charges against them <sup>[N6](#n6)</sup>.
5. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused-item-5"></a>**The Bangladesh Embassy in Doha** advises on going to the labour court or the online platform; its numbers are in Work visa: company worker, If you aren't paid, are abused or your passport is taken, item 8 <sup>[162 (2021)](#o162)</sup>.
6. <a id="if-you-arent-paid-or-are-abused-item-6"></a>**From Bangladesh:** the worker or a close relative can report harassment of a woman worker abroad to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd, with her passport, smart card and RP copies and the agency's details; it is free and dealt with in 7 working days <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>.

**No Bangladesh safe house in Qatar**
WEWB runs safe houses only in Saudi Arabia, Oman, Jordan, Libya and Lebanon <sup>[176](#o176)</sup>. For those countries a family applies free to WEWB, directly or through the expatriate welfare section of the DC's office or the DEMO, and WEWB writes to the embassy; call centre 16135, +8809610102030 from abroad <sup>[176](#o176)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does WEWB act on a family's request for a worker in Qatar? <sup>[176](#o176)</sup>

<a id="if-your-employer-reports-you-absent-absconding-1"></a>

#### 4.14.2 If your employer reports you absent ("absconding")

A household sponsor can file a free "absconding" report against a domestic worker at MOI's Search and Follow-up Department, an MOI service centre or MOI's app, and you contest it as in Work visa: company worker, If your employer reports you absent, What it can lead to, item 2 <sup>[48](#o48)</sup>. Women who flee abuse can face absconding or theft charges in retaliation <sup>[N53 (2020)](#n53)</sup>.

<a id="changing-employer-or-leaving-1"></a>

### 4.15 Changing employer or leaving

<a id="ending-the-job-and-changing-employer"></a>

#### 4.15.1 Ending the job and changing employer

The employer may dismiss you without notice or that year's end-of-service pay if you break the law or the contract <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. You may leave early and keep full end-of-service pay if the employer breaks the law or the contract, lied about the terms, assaults you (or a family member does), or ignores a serious known danger <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15)</sup>. You no longer need your employer's NOC to change employer <sup>[172 (2020)](#o172), [11](#o11)</sup>: you notify MOL, with an RP that is valid or expired less than 90 days, as in Work visa: company worker (see [If you want to change employer](#if-you-want-to-change-employer)) <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does MOL's change-of-employer e-service accept domestic workers, logging in under "Labors and Individuals"? <sup>[N109](#n109), [47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>
> - What notice must a domestic worker give when changing employer? <sup>[47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>

<a id="leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-and-going-home-on-leave"></a>

#### 4.15.2 Leaving at the end of the job and going home on leave

You may leave without the employer's permission, but must tell the employer at least 72 hours before you go <sup>[11](#o11)</sup>.

Leave, tickets and end-of-service pay are in Work visa: domestic worker, Your rights at work (Hours, rest and leave; Tickets and end-of-service pay) <sup>[15 (2017)](#o15), [11](#o11)</sup>. Before flying, pay any fines and declare cash or gold worth QR 50,000 or more, as in Work visa: company worker ([Leaving at the end of the job, item 6: Clear fines and travel bans](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-6); [Leaving at the end of the job, item 7: Declare cash and gold](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-7)), which also says how long you may stay after your RP ends ([Leaving at the end of the job, item 5: Leave in time](#leaving-at-the-end-of-the-job-item-5)) <sup>[N112](#n112), [166 (2019)](#o166)</sup>.

<a id="tourist-visa-hayya-a1"></a>

## 5 Tourist visa: Hayya A1

A Bangladeshi visiting Qatar for tourism or to see friends and family needs the Hayya Tourist Visa (A1) before travelling: Bangladesh is on none of MOI's visa-free or on-arrival lists (see [Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[1](#o1), [52](#o52), [55](#o55), [56](#o56), [57](#o57), [60](#o60)</sup>. Apply on Hayya, at hayya.qa or in the "Hayya to Qatar" app <sup>[1](#o1), [177](#o177), [178](#o178)</sup>. A Bangladeshi living in Bahrain, Kuwait, Oman, Saudi Arabia or the UAE applies for the A2 (GCC Resident Visa) instead, and one who lives in, or holds a valid visa for, the Schengen area, UK, US, Australia, New Zealand or Canada for the A3 (Visa with ETA, Electronic Travel Authorization) (see Hayya A2, A3 and A4 (if you live in a Gulf country, hold certain visas or travel with a Gulf citizen)) <sup>[1](#o1), [52](#o52), [178](#o178)</sup>. The A1 never allows work (see [Can you work?](#can-you-work)) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="cost-and-time"></a>

### 5.1 Cost and time

The fee is QR 100, paid inside Hayya only after final approval and within 14 days of the status changing to "Pending (Payment)", or the application is cancelled; it is not refunded whatever happens to the trip, and it can be waived under certain conditions <sup>[52](#o52), [179](#o179), [1](#o1)</sup>. Compulsory health insurance adds QR 50 per person per month ([Step 1: Prepare your documents and insurance](#step-1-prepare-your-documents-and-insurance)) <sup>[180 (2023)](#o180), [181](#o181)</sup>. Hayya has no fixed processing time <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - When is the A1 fee waived? <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>

<a id="step-1-prepare-your-documents-and-insurance"></a>

### 5.2 Step 1: Prepare your documents and insurance

Your personal details and the name on your hotel booking must match your passport exactly <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>. You'll need:

- **A passport valid at least 3 months from the day you arrive** <sup>[52](#o52), [179](#o179), [182 (2023)](#o182)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Do airlines or airport officers hold Hayya visitors to the 6 months' validity from the visa date that MOI's residence regulations and visa-conditions page require (waivable by the authority)? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [183](#o183)</sup>

- **A scan of the passport's photo page and a digital photo** in JPG or JPEG, showing only your face and close to your passport photo <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>.
- **A hotel booking for every night,** from arrival to departure, matching the flight dates with no gaps, in your passport name and showing the hotel's address <sup>[52](#o52), [179](#o179)</sup>. Once you add the booking proof it can't be deleted or changed <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>. Hayya may exempt some applicants from accommodation proof, or require them to book through Discover Qatar (see [Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. For visa on arrival, booking through Discover Qatar is required of India, Pakistan, Thailand and Iran passports, not Bangladesh, and Qatar may add others <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>. If you book through Discover Qatar and the visa is refused, it charges QR 100 to cancel <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Must Bangladeshis book the A1 hotel through Discover Qatar? <sup>[58](#o58)</sup>
  > - Must the booking proof be a PDF under 2 MB? <sup>[N119](#n119)</sup>
  > - Can another booking proof be uploaded if one is rejected? <sup>[N119](#n119)</sup>

- **Or a host in Qatar** ("Host Family & Friends") instead of a hotel: any Qatar resident with a valid QID and an address registered on Metrash can host, with 1 property and up to 5 guests <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Being hosted doesn't guarantee approval <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Must the host first upload, in his own Hayya account, his QID, the property address, guest details and a title deed or rental agreement? <sup>[N119](#n119)</sup>

- **A return flight ticket** <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Book the flight only after approval; any loss from travelling before the e-visa is issued is your own <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Airlines expect the return date to be within 30 days of entry <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>.
- **Visitor health insurance for the whole stay.** MVHI (Mandatory Visitor Health Insurance) is compulsory: no entry visa is issued or renewed without proof of cover for the visa period, and MOI requires it to enter Qatar <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132), [181](#o181), [178](#o178)</sup>.
  - Buy it from an insurer registered with MoPH or an MoPH-approved international insurer; MoPH's website lists the approved insurance <sup>[181](#o181), [52](#o52)</sup>.
  - It costs QR 50 per person per month, the same when you extend <sup>[180 (2023)](#o180), [181](#o181)</sup>.
  - Make the cover start on the day you enter; it can't be cancelled once it starts <sup>[184 (2022)](#o184)</sup>. International health insurance that includes Qatar is accepted instead <sup>[184 (2022)](#o184)</sup>.
  - It covers emergencies up to QR 150,000, emergency assistance (ambulance, evacuation) up to QR 35,000 and repatriation of remains up to QR 10,000, with no pre-approval, co-payment or deductible; non-emergency treatment is not covered, and you pay its full cost <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>. MoPH's health insurance helpline is 16000, extension 1; from abroad +974 4406 9963 <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>.

  > **Need to know**
  > - Which insurers are on MoPH's current approved list? <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>

- **For children:** each visitor needs their own e-visa; children under 18 can be added as dependents, and the A1 has no age limit <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya"></a>

### 5.3 Step 2: Apply on Hayya

The application has six stages: Select Visa, Personal Information, Validate Information, Accommodation Confirmation, Payment and Hayya E-Visa <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-1"></a>**Open an account** at [hayya.qa](https://hayya.qa) or in the "Hayya to Qatar" app: sign up with your email, verify it, then set a password <sup>[1](#o1), [179](#o179)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-2"></a>**Select Visa:** choose "Tourist Visa (A1)" <sup>[185](#o185)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-3"></a>**Personal Information:** type your details exactly as in the passport and upload the passport and your photo <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-4"></a>**Validate Information:** re-check every field Hayya fills in automatically, or the application may be rejected <sup>[179](#o179)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-5"></a>**Confirm your accommodation:** the application first shows "Pending"; after the authorities approve, it moves to accommodation confirmation, where you upload the hotel booking or choose "Host Family & Friends" <sup>[58](#o58), [52](#o52)</sup>. Do it within 30 days of the "Pending Accommodation Confirmation" email, or the application is cancelled <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.
6. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-6"></a>**Pay** QR 100 within 14 days of "Pending (Payment)" <sup>[52](#o52), [179](#o179)</sup>.
7. <a id="step-2-apply-on-hayya-item-7"></a>**Download the e-visa:** once fully approved, a single-entry e-visa is emailed to you; download the "Entry Visa", which carries an Entry Reference Number (Hayya Number) <sup>[52](#o52), [179](#o179)</sup>. Without it you won't be let in, and the old printed Hayya Card is no longer valid for entry <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Then book the flight <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="what-youll-see"></a>

#### 5.3.1 What you'll see

The statuses are Pending Submission, Pending, Pending (Ticket Verification), Pending Accommodation Confirmation, Pending (Payment), Pending Entry Visa, Approved and Rejected; a pending application does not allow travel <sup>[179](#o179), [52](#o52)</sup>. Applications have stayed at "Pending" for 10 days to 3 weeks, and only the Hayya helpline could advise on their status <sup>[N120](#n120), [N119](#n119)</sup>. You can't start a second application while one is pending <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Hayya support: 2022 inside Qatar, +974 4441 2022 from abroad, info@hayya.qa <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="changes-after-approval"></a>

#### 5.3.2 Changes after approval

You can edit an approved application free of charge until its "Last Entry Date" passes, while you are outside Qatar; it then goes back to "Pending" for re-approval <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. If you renew your passport after the e-visa is issued, update the application with the new passport and travel with the updated e-visa <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-check-your-visa-is-real"></a>

### 5.4 Step 3: Check your visa is real

Any of these works:

- **On Hayya:** open "Track your E-Visa" at [hayya.qa/en/visa-inquiry](https://hayya.qa/en/visa-inquiry) and enter the Entry Reference Number, or your passport number and nationality <sup>[113](#o113)</sup>.
- **On MOI's Visa Inquiry,** as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1: On MOI's Visa Inquiry](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1); it looks up issued visit visas by visa number and lets you print them <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.
- **By phone:** Ami Probashi checks Qatar visas through its helpline 16768 for a small fee and replies by SMS whether the visa is valid <sup>[N92](#n92)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-travel-and-arrival-at-hamad-airport"></a>

### 5.5 Step 4: Travel and arrival at Hamad airport

1. <a id="step-4-travel-and-arrival-at-hamad-airport-item-1"></a>**Carry** your passport, the e-visa (printed, or in the app), your return ticket and your accommodation proof; accommodation can be checked at any point during the trip <sup>[52](#o52), [182 (2023)](#o182)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-travel-and-arrival-at-hamad-airport-item-2"></a>**Travel before the "Last Entry Date"** shown on the e-visa; a visa issued in advance must be used within 90 days of issue <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [52](#o52)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-travel-and-arrival-at-hamad-airport-item-3"></a>**Give your fingerprints:** they must be taken before you enter Qatar on any visa <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Hamad International Airport (HIA, Doha's airport) has 76 free e-gates (automatic immigration gates where you scan your passport and go through) for citizens, residents and visitors, for eligible passengers including children taller than 130 cm <sup>[N112](#n112), [186](#o186)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - May first-time Bangladeshi A1 visitors use the e-gates, or only people who have entered Qatar before? <sup>[187](#o187), [182 (2023)](#o182)</sup>
   > - Does Qatar have an arrival card? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>

4. <a id="step-4-travel-and-arrival-at-hamad-airport-item-4"></a>**If someone hosts you,** they must report your name and address to the local security department within 24 hours of arrival (a hotel must do the same), and the host is legally responsible for you <sup>[188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>.

**If you arrive without a valid passport or visa,** the airline must return you, at its own cost, to the country you came from or your home country <sup>[188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>.

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay"></a>

### 5.6 How long you can stay

The A1 is single entry and lets you stay 30 days <sup>[179](#o179), [52](#o52)</sup>. You may not stay longer unless the visa is renewed (Law 21 of 2015, Article 11) <sup>[188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>. Overstaying a visit is a crime, settled at QR 200 a day up to QR 12,000 (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[49 (2015)](#o49), [188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>.

**A baby born during the visit:** this is covered separately (see [Having a child in Qatar](#having-a-child-in-qatar)) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="extending-your-stay"></a>

#### 5.6.1 Extending your stay

1. <a id="extending-your-stay-item-1"></a>**Apply before the e-visa expires** at the Directorate of Passport at HIA, where all visa extensions are processed <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.
2. <a id="extending-your-stay-item-2"></a>**Buy visitor insurance for the extra time,** at QR 50 a month ([Step 1: Prepare your documents and insurance](#step-1-prepare-your-documents-and-insurance)) <sup>[181](#o181), [180 (2023)](#o180)</sup>.

Some visitors say MOI's online extension failed for them, and one said buying 60 days of insurance extended the A1 automatically <sup>[N120](#n120)</sup>. Many visitors have extended by 30 days <sup>[N121](#n121)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can A1 holders extend on MOI's online Visit Visa Extension form (MOI Services > Inquiries > Visa Services > Visit Visa Extension: visa or passport number, nationality, picture code, "Extend")? <sup>[189](#o189), [112](#o112)</sup>
> - What does an A1 extension cost? <sup>[N121](#n121)</sup>
> - What is the longest total stay on an extended A1? <sup>[N121](#n121)</sup>

<a id="can-you-work"></a>

### 5.7 Can you work?

A Hayya visitor may not work in Qatar by law; a work visa is granted only under an employment contract with the employer bringing you in <sup>[52](#o52), [188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>. No one may employ a foreigner without a work contract, and a visitor may not work for an entity not authorised <sup>[188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>. Breaching the purpose of entry or overstaying is a crime, and you may not leave Qatar until the case is settled; the penalties are in What can get you jailed or deported <sup>[188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>. Whether a visit visa can be changed into a work visa inside Qatar (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[N62 (2023)](#n62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does Bangladesh airport immigration specially screen first-time tourist-visa travellers to Qatar? <sup>[N122 (2012)](#n122)</sup>

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused"></a>

### 5.8 If the visa is refused

The reason shows on your Hayya dashboard, though sometimes none is given, and approval is at the authorities' discretion <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. A corrected application can be re-submitted; one stayed pending about 10 days after re-submission <sup>[N119](#n119)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can an A1 refusal be appealed? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>

<a id="scams-2"></a>

### 5.9 Scams

**A visit or Hayya visa "with a job."** A broker sells a tourist or Hayya visa as a work visa, charging high fees with false job promises <sup>[N62 (2023)](#n62), [N123 (2023)](#n123)</sup>, but a Hayya visitor may not work by law <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. In 2014 brokers, helped by Bangladeshi recruiting agencies, took Tk 3 to 3.5 lakh each from 23 Bangladeshis and brought them to Qatar on tourist visas; they found no work for three months, their visas expired and they became illegal <sup>[N124 (2014)](#n124)</sup>. Check the visa as in [Step 3: Check your visa is real](#step-3-check-your-visa-is-real); a job needs a work visa ([Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 5, item 3: Check it is a work visa](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-3)), and check any job offer as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 1, item 4](#step-1-prepare-your-documents-and-insurance) <sup>[113](#o113), [83](#o83), [52](#o52)</sup>.

**"Free visa."** See Work visa: company worker, Scams, "Free visa" or a bought visa; the penalties are covered separately (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[N8](#n8), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="fake-visa-papers"></a>

#### 5.9.1 Fake visa papers

In 2019 a Dhaka gang showed job seekers fake offer letters, contracts and even a fake Qatar visa (see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), Scams, Fake job adverts and fake papers) <sup>[N42 (2019)](#n42)</sup>. A genuine visa shows on Hayya's "Track your E-Visa" or MOI's Visa Inquiry ([Step 3: Check your visa is real](#step-3-check-your-visa-is-real)) <sup>[113](#o113), [83](#o83)</sup>.

<a id="fake-websites-and-paid-agents"></a>

#### 5.9.2 Fake websites and paid agents

Hayya runs on hayya.qa and the "Hayya to Qatar" app; its fee is QR 100, paid inside Hayya after approval, and the account is opened with an email address and a password ([Step 2: Apply on Hayya](#step-2-apply-on-hayya), items 1 and 6) <sup>[1](#o1), [52](#o52), [179](#o179)</sup>. For fake government sites, see [Work visa: company worker](#work-visa-company-worker), [Step 5: Check the visa yourself](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself) (after item 4) <sup>[N93](#n93)</sup>.

<a id="moi-visit-visa"></a>

### 5.10 MOI visit visa

A regular visit visa issued by MOI is still an option besides the A1, depending on your profile and purpose <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does the Qatar Embassy in Dhaka issue MOI visit visas to Bangladeshis? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>
> - Does the Qatar Embassy in Dhaka help with Hayya applications? <sup>[1](#o1)</sup>

<a id="family-residence-spouse-children-and-parents"></a>

## 6 Family residence (spouse, children and parents)

A resident of Qatar can bring a spouse and children to live with them on a family residence permit if the resident's job and salary meet MOI's rules <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148), [3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="who-can-apply"></a>

### 6.1 Who can apply

<a id="who-qualifies-1"></a>

#### 6.1.1 Who qualifies

A private-sector sponsor needs a technical or specialised occupation and a salary of at least QR 10,000, or QR 6,000 with family housing, shown in the employment contract <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>. A government or semi-government employee needs family housing from the employer or a salary of at least QR 10,000 <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a driver, delivery worker, cleaner or other labour-category worker sponsor family at all, whatever the salary? <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148), [N125](#n125)</sup>
> - Does MOI check the salary through the employer's WPS transfers? <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148), [N125](#n125)</sup>
> - Does MOI check the education through the labour contract on file? <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148), [N125](#n125)</sup>
> - Does MOI give a "missing documents" result when WPS or contract data is missing? <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148), [N125](#n125)</sup>
> - Has MOI changed the salary rule since December 2023? <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>
> - Are family residence and visit visas restricted for some nationalities, as reported in November 2025, and is Bangladesh one? <sup>[N125](#n125)</sup>

<a id="who-can-come"></a>

#### 6.1.2 Who can come

A resident can bring the spouse, sons up to 25 who are still studying, and unmarried daughters <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>; the Minister of Interior or his delegate can make an exception to the sons' age limit <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Children aged 6 to 18 must be in a licensed school in Qatar, or show enrolment abroad through the education ministry's platform, to get or renew an RP <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>.

<a id="more-than-one-wife"></a>

#### 6.1.3 More than one wife

The authorities may give residence to more than one wife if the husband has acceptable reasons and can afford to support two families <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="parents"></a>

#### 6.1.4 Parents

A resident's parents can get residence only with the approval of the Minister of Interior or his delegate, if he sees a reason for it, and the resident must meet the same work-residence, salary and housing conditions as for a spouse and children <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="family-of-a-qatari-citizen"></a>

#### 6.1.5 Family of a Qatari citizen

The non-Qatari spouse and the children of a Qatari citizen can get family residence under Qatar's law on marriage to foreigners, and the Qatari's non-Qatari parents can get residence on proof of the relationship <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. A foreign wife of a Qatari can get residence on her minor Qatari son if her husband dies or they divorce <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a resident apply for residence for a second wife, with which documents and fees? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - How does a resident apply for residence for parents, with which documents and fees? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - How does a resident apply for residence for the family of a Qatari, with which documents and fees? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>

<a id="cost-and-time-1"></a>

### 6.2 Cost and time

- Family residence visa: QR 200 <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>
- Medical Commission exam: QR 100 per family member <sup>[121 (2021)](#o121)</sup>
- RP (on the QID card): QR 400 per family member <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>
- Attesting the family visa at the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha: QR 50 per person <sup>[173 (2021)](#o173)</sup>

No RP is granted without proof of compulsory health insurance <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What does health insurance for a resident's family members cost? <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>

<a id="step-1-check-you-qualify-and-gather-the-papers"></a>

### 6.3 Step 1: Check you qualify and gather the papers

1. <a id="step-1-check-you-qualify-and-gather-the-papers-item-1"></a>**Check your contract** shows the occupation and salary required (see [Who qualifies](#who-qualifies-1)) <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-check-you-qualify-and-gather-the-papers-item-2"></a>**Gather the papers MOI's Family Visa form asks for** <sup>[191](#o191), [190](#o190)</sup>:
   - your ID copy
   - the passport copy of each person you bring
   - the certified marriage contract and children's birth certificates ([Step 2: Attest the marriage and birth certificates](#step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates))
   - your certified employment contract showing job and salary ([Step 3: Get your employment contract attested](#step-3-get-your-employment-contract-attested))
   - a police clearance (good-conduct certificate) for each adult ([Step 4: Police clearance and medical for adult family members](#step-4-police-clearance-and-medical-for-adult-family-members))
   - health insurance for the whole stay
   - a medical exam done outside Qatar ([Step 4: Police clearance and medical for adult family members](#step-4-police-clearance-and-medical-for-adult-family-members))

<a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates"></a>

### 6.4 Step 2: Attest the marriage and birth certificates

1. <a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates-item-1"></a>**In Bangladesh,** have the marriage (nikah) and birth certificates attested by the Law Ministry, then MOFA in Dhaka, as for certificates in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 3](#step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates-item-2"></a>**Then have them legalised for Qatar,** through QVC Dhaka's attestation counter or Qatar MOFA's "Complete Attestation Outside Qatar" service, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 3](#step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates) <sup>[35](#o35), [104](#o104)</sup>. In practice they are attested by the Qatar Embassy in Bangladesh and then by Qatar MOFA in Doha <sup>[N125](#n125)</sup>. The Bangladesh Embassy in Doha also attests certificates already attested by MOFA in Dhaka (fees in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 3](#step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates)) <sup>[106](#o106)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-get-your-employment-contract-attested"></a>

### 6.5 Step 3: Get your employment contract attested
