1. <a id="step-3-get-your-employment-contract-attested-item-1"></a>**Have the contract attested;** it must show your profession and salary <sup>[190](#o190), [191](#o191)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Where does a resident get the contract attested for a family visa? <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>
   > - What does attesting the contract for a family visa cost? <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>

<a id="step-4-police-clearance-and-medical-for-adult-family-members"></a>

### 6.6 Step 4: Police clearance and medical for adult family members

1. <a id="step-4-police-clearance-and-medical-for-adult-family-members-item-1"></a>**Get each adult a police clearance** as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 2: Then have them legalised for Qatar](#step-2-attest-the-marriage-and-birth-certificates-item-2), attested by MOFA in Dhaka and then by the Qatar Embassy <sup>[191](#o191), [51 (2021)](#o51), [8](#o8)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-police-clearance-and-medical-for-adult-family-members-item-2"></a>**Have each adult do a medical test outside Qatar** <sup>[190](#o190), [191](#o191)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Where in Bangladesh do family members do this medical? <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>
   > - What does this medical cost? <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>

<a id="step-5-apply"></a>

### 6.7 Step 5: Apply

1. <a id="step-5-apply-item-1"></a>**Apply at an MOI Services Centre, or on MOI's website or Metrash** <sup>[190](#o190), [148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-5-apply-item-2"></a>**In Metrash,** choose Visa, then Family Resident Visa; type the names in Arabic, and upload JPG files of at most 500 KB, not PDF <sup>[N125](#n125)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-5-apply-item-3"></a>**On paper,** type MOI's form in Arabic, with names as in the passport and attachments in order, and bring the originals at registration <sup>[191](#o191)</sup>.
4. <a id="step-5-apply-item-4"></a>**Pay the visa fee** (see [Cost and time](#cost-and-time-1)) <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>.
5. <a id="step-5-apply-item-5"></a>**Wait.** There is no fixed processing time: it takes days to months, and after the visa number is issued security clearance takes about a week <sup>[N125](#n125)</sup>.

<a id="step-6-check-and-print-the-visa"></a>

### 6.8 Step 6: Check and print the visa

1. <a id="step-6-check-and-print-the-visa-item-1"></a>**Follow the application** on MOI's Visa Inquiry by application number (with your QID number and the application date), or look up the issued visa by visa number and print it, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1: Apply at an MOI Services Centre, or on MOI's website or Metrash](#step-5-apply-item-1) <sup>[83](#o83), [112](#o112)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does Visa Inquiry show family residence visas, or only work and visit visas? <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>

2. <a id="step-6-check-and-print-the-visa-item-2"></a>**Use it within 90 days:** a visa issued in advance is valid for use for 90 days <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-6-check-and-print-the-visa-item-3"></a>**If you want the visa attested,** the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha does it with copies of the visa, the passports and the nikahnama (marriage contract), in 3 working days (fee: see [Cost and time](#cost-and-time-1)) <sup>[173 (2021)](#o173)</sup>.

<a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-1"></a>

### 6.9 Step 7: After you arrive

1. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-1-item-1"></a>**RP and QID within 30 days.** Each family member must do the medical check at the Medical Commission and give fingerprints, and you get each one's RP; a fine runs from day 31 <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [121 (2021)](#o121), [38 (2021)](#o38), [37](#o37)</sup>.
   1. At the Medical Commission bring the original passport, a clear copy of the RP with a mobile number for emergencies, and the medical certificate from the accredited centre in Bangladesh, and pay by card (fees: see [Cost and time](#cost-and-time-1)) <sup>[121 (2021)](#o121)</sup>.
   2. Then issue each RP on MOI's e-service "Issue/Reactivate RP by Visa Number": log in with your smart card registered in Metrash, choose personal sponsorship, and enter the visa number, the person's details, address and residence period <sup>[38 (2021)](#o38)</sup>.
   3. Choose to collect the card at the Expatriates' Affairs Department or have it sent by Qatar Post, and pay the RP fee <sup>[38 (2021)](#o38), [122](#o122)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-1-item-2"></a>**School.** Enrol children aged 6 to 18 in a licensed school <sup>[148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-7-after-you-arrive-1-item-3"></a>**A baby born in Qatar** needs its RP applied for within 90 days of birth (see [Having a child in Qatar](#having-a-child-in-qatar)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-1"></a>

### 6.10 Can you work?

A wife can take a job while her residence stays under the head of the family <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. A husband sponsored by his wife may not work until his residence is moved to the employer <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Changing from family to work residence is covered separately (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[N126](#n126)</sup>.

**If the marriage ends,** a family member may move to another sponsor with the authority's approval <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-visa-is-refused-1"></a>

### 6.11 If the visa is refused

The sponsor can go to the Immigration Office with all the originals and ask to meet the visa approval committee <sup>[N125](#n125)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is there an official appeal against a refused family residence visa? <sup>[190](#o190)</sup>

<a id="scams-3"></a>

### 6.12 Scams

Any family visa can be checked by its number on MOI's Visa Inquiry ([Step 6, item 1: Follow the application](#step-6-check-and-print-the-visa-item-1)) <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What family-visa scams are reported? <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>

<a id="family-visit-visa"></a>

## 7 Family visit visa

A resident can host relatives on MOI's family visit visa for one month, extendable by up to five more months for first-degree relatives <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>. The host applies from Qatar <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

<a id="who-can-apply-1"></a>

### 7.1 Who can apply

<a id="who-can-host"></a>

#### 7.1.1 Who can host

The host must be a resident who meets all of these <sup>[78](#o78), [148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>:

- a profession outside the labour category
- a salary of QR 5,000 a month under an accredited employment contract
- family accommodation in their own name, accredited by the authorities

A visit may be refused if the profession on the host's QID is labour-type, though an officer at an MOI Service Centre can change the decision <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>.

<a id="who-can-visit"></a>

#### 7.1.2 Who can visit

The visitor can be a first-degree relative (father, mother, spouse, children), and also siblings, grandparents, grandchildren, nephews and nieces, uncles and aunts <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

<a id="cost-and-time-2"></a>

### 7.2 Cost and time

The visa is QR 200 and each month's extension QR 200 <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>, plus visitor health insurance, priced as in Tourist visa: Hayya A1, [Step 1: Prepare the papers](#step-1-prepare-the-papers) (Visitor health insurance for the whole stay) <sup>[180 (2023)](#o180)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-prepare-the-papers"></a>

### 7.3 Step 1: Prepare the papers

1. <a id="step-1-prepare-the-papers-item-1"></a>**Check the visitor's passport** is valid at least 6 months <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-prepare-the-papers-item-2"></a>**Get proof of the relationship** ready to upload <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>. Attestation, where needed, is done as in Family residence (spouse, children and parents), [Step 2: Apply on Metrash and pay](#step-2-apply-on-metrash-and-pay) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - Does MOI now require an attested marriage certificate for a family visit? <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>
   > - Do children's birth certificates for a family visit need no attestation? <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>

3. <a id="step-1-prepare-the-papers-item-3"></a>**Get proof of family accommodation** in the host's name <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

<a id="step-2-apply-on-metrash-and-pay"></a>

### 7.4 Step 2: Apply on Metrash and pay

1. <a id="step-2-apply-on-metrash-and-pay-item-1"></a>**The host applies in Metrash,** uploading the visitor's passport, the proof of relationship and the proof of accommodation <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-apply-on-metrash-and-pay-item-2"></a>**Pay the visa fee** (see [Cost and time](#cost-and-time-2)) <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-buy-health-insurance"></a>

### 7.5 Step 3: Buy health insurance

1. <a id="step-3-buy-health-insurance-item-1"></a>**Buy visitor health insurance (MVHI) for the whole visit,** as in Tourist visa: Hayya A1, [Step 1: Prepare the papers](#step-1-prepare-the-papers) (Visitor health insurance for the whole stay); no visa is issued without it <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132), [78](#o78)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-3-buy-health-insurance-item-2"></a>**Use the SMS link:** after approval the host gets an SMS link to buy it online <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel"></a>

### 7.6 Step 4: Check the visa and travel

1. <a id="step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-1"></a>**Check and print the visa** on MOI's Visa Inquiry, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1: On MOI's Visa Inquiry](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1) <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-2"></a>**Travel within 90 days:** a visa issued in advance is valid for use for 90 days <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does MOI take to decide on a family visit visa? <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>
> - Can a family visit visa refusal be appealed? <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>

<a id="how-long-you-can-stay-1"></a>

### 7.7 How long you can stay

<a id="extending-the-visit"></a>

#### 7.7.1 Extending the visit

First-degree relatives can stay up to 5 more months, at QR 200 a month <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>.

1. <a id="extending-the-visit-item-1"></a>**Do the medical check** before the allowed period ends, if the visitor wants to stay longer than a month <sup>[192](#o192)</sup>.
2. <a id="extending-the-visit-item-2"></a>**Then apply.** Extend online on MOI's "Visit Visa Extension" service: enter the visa or passport number, the nationality and the verification code, then press "Extend" <sup>[189](#o189)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long can relatives who aren't first-degree (siblings, grandparents and others) extend to? <sup>[78](#o78)</sup>
> - Does the extension medical cost QR 100? <sup>[N127](#n127)</sup>
> - Must a family visit visa instead be extended at the Directorate of Passport at HIA, where all visa extensions are processed? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>

<a id="a-baby-born-during-the-visit"></a>

#### 7.7.2 A baby born during the visit

See Having a child in Qatar, [Step 4: Check the visa and travel](#step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="can-you-work-2"></a>

### 7.8 Can you work?

Overstaying is a crime, settled at QR 200 a day up to QR 12,000, and so is breaching the purpose of entry, for example by working (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[192](#o192), [49 (2015)](#o49), [188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>. Overstay fines can be paid in Metrash before going to the airport <sup>[N112](#n112)</sup>. The authority may, at its discretion, change a visa's purpose inside Qatar while the visa is valid (see [Changing visa type](#changing-visa-type)) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="scams-4"></a>

### 7.9 Scams

Any visa can be checked by its number on MOI's Visa Inquiry ([Step 4, item 1: Check and print the visa](#step-4-check-the-visa-and-travel-item-1)) <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What family-visit-visa and personal-visit-visa scams are reported? <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>

<a id="personal-visit-visa"></a>

### 7.10 Personal visit visa

A separate personal visit visa lets a resident host a visitor in cases the authority decides <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [193](#o193)</sup>. MOI issues it only with the approval of the Minister of Interior or his representative, for one month, extendable by one more, at QR 200 a month <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>. The host applies at an MOI Services Centre with:

- the form <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [193](#o193)</sup>
- a copy of the visitor's travel document <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [193](#o193)</sup>
- the host's ID or RP <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [193](#o193)</sup>
- proof of financial ability: a bank statement or work contract <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [193](#o193)</sup>
- proof of family residence facilities <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>

MOI also asks for a medical test if the visitor stays more than 30 days <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does MOI grant the personal visit visa to Bangladeshis? <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>
> - How long does MOI take to decide on a personal visit visa? <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>
> - Can a personal visit visa refusal be appealed? <sup>[193](#o193)</sup>

<a id="business-visit"></a>

## 8 Business visit

A business visitor doesn't apply on Hayya: Hayya's business service (the 60-day Official Visa) is only for invitations from government and semi-government bodies, and the businessmen visit visa is applied for through licensed companies or a service provider <sup>[52](#o52), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. It is for business people coming for consultations, deals, exchange of expertise or investment (residence regulations, MOI Decision 25 of 2019, Article 40) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Tourist and business visit visas are valid for one trip of 30 or 60 days, or multiple entry for 6 months or 1 year with at most 90 days per trip (Article 30) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. A business visa does not allow work for a Qatari employer <sup>[188 (2020)](#o188)</sup>, and no entry visa is issued without proof of health insurance (see [Tourist visa: Hayya A1](#tourist-visa-hayya-a1), [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job) (Visitor health insurance for the whole stay)) <sup>[132 (2021)](#o132)</sup>.

<a id="getting-the-visa"></a>

### 8.1 Getting the visa

For MOI's "Businessmen Visa", a Qatari trading or investment company with prior approval applies at an MOI Services Centre through its representative or owner; the fee is QR 200 <sup>[194](#o194)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [194](#o194)</sup>:
- a valid passport (the company submits a clear copy);
- proof you are a businessman;
- a hotel booking or accommodation;
- a return ticket.

<a id="meeting-or-work-mission"></a>

### 8.2 Meeting or work mission

A visit visa for a meeting or work mission at a government body's request lasts one month, extendable to three; at the request of a company or other non-government body it needs a hotel booking or accommodation and a return ticket (Article 42) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="training-course"></a>

### 8.3 Training course

For a course run by an accredited body in Qatar, the host or the visitor applies with these (Article 43) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>:
- the course registration or acceptance letter;
- the course and organiser details;
- a valid travel document;
- the organiser's registration licence;
- a hotel booking or accommodation;
- a return ticket.

MOI's 72-hour business visa on arrival is not open to Bangladeshis (see [Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[60](#o60), [61](#o61)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the online route for a Bangladeshi business, meeting or training visitor? <sup>[194](#o194), [195](#o195)</sup>
> - What is the processing time for a Bangladeshi business, meeting or training visitor? <sup>[194](#o194), [195](#o195)</sup>
> - What is the appeal route for a Bangladeshi business, meeting or training visitor? <sup>[194](#o194), [195](#o195)</sup>
> - Can a business visitor bring a spouse? <sup>[194](#o194), [195](#o195)</sup>
> - What scams are reported around business visas? <sup>[194](#o194), [195](#o195)</sup>

<a id="temporary-work-visa-and-work-visit"></a>

## 9 Temporary work visa and work visit

Only an employer in Qatar can apply for these short work visas; a worker can't apply alone <sup>[16 (2019)](#o16), [196](#o196)</sup>. Check any such visa as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1: On MOI's Visa Inquiry](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1) <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.

<a id="temporary-work-visa"></a>

### 9.1 Temporary work visa

This visa is for temporary work of at most six months, for private companies and other employers (MOI Decision 92 of 2019); the employer applies with <sup>[16 (2019)](#o16)</sup>:
- the form;
- a copy of a valid passport;
- a medical test and fingerprints;
- an authenticated work contract;
- the establishment's registration card.

MOL approves requests free, and only for <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>:
- government and semi-government bodies;
- industry and energy companies;
- factories with government contracts;
- companies with contracts with the government or energy companies;
- establishments MOL authorises.

> **Need to know**
> - Are Bangladeshis recruited on temporary work visas? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>
> - Does BMET clear Bangladeshis for temporary work visas? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>
> - What is MOI's visa fee for a temporary work visa? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>
> - What is MOI's processing time for a temporary work visa? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>
> - Is there an appeal if a temporary work visa is refused? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>
> - Can a temporary work visa holder bring a spouse? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>
> - What scams are reported around temporary work visas? <sup>[197 (2022)](#o197)</sup>

<a id="multiple-entry-work-visit-visa"></a>

### 9.2 Multiple Entry Work Visit Visa

This visa is for workers in professions Qatar needs, brought in by government bodies or companies, valid for several trips <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Private companies pay QR 200 a month; government bodies pay nothing <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>. The employer needs <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>:
- RARC's approval;
- a copy of the sponsor's establishment ID;
- a copy of your passport;
- proof of your profession or qualification;
- a medical test, if you will stay more than 30 days; no foreigner may stay in Qatar more than 30 days without one <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does it last one month, extendable to at most six, for "rare professions" <sup>[196](#o196)</sup>, or six months, extendable for a similar period (residence regulations, Article 48) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>?
> - Is RARC the same body as MOI's Committee Reviewing Recruitment Applications (see [Work visa: domestic worker](#work-visa-domestic-worker)), which another MOI page calls the Recruitment Applications Reviewing Committee? <sup>[196](#o196), [108](#o108), [198](#o198)</sup>
> - Are Bangladeshis issued the Multiple Entry Work Visit Visa? <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Does BMET clear the Multiple Entry Work Visit Visa? <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - How long does the Multiple Entry Work Visit Visa take? <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Is there an appeal if it is refused? <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Can the holder bring a spouse? <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - What scams are reported around the Multiple Entry Work Visit Visa? <sup>[196](#o196), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>

<a id="seafarers-and-truck-drivers"></a>

### 9.3 Seafarers and truck drivers

The seafarers' entry visa is for seafarers and workers on temporary tasks on ships and marine or land installations in oil or gas, for one month, extendable to six months, with a valid seaman's passport (Article 50) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The seafarers' transit visa is for seafarers joining ships anchored in Qatar's waters, for 48 hours (Article 51) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

Truck drivers and their helpers get a visa through import-export companies, transport and shipping offices or licensed goods carriers, for several trips within three months, one week per trip (Article 52) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Truck drivers resident in a GCC country get it on arrival at land border points if their GCC residence is valid at least six months: one trip of a week, or several trips within three months of a week each, extendable by a week; they leave by land, or exceptionally by the airport (Article 53) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Are the seafarer and truck-driver visas issued to Bangladeshis? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Where are the seafarer and truck-driver visas applied for? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - What do the seafarer and truck-driver visas cost? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Is there an appeal if a seafarer or truck-driver visa is refused? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - What scams are reported around the seafarer and truck-driver visas? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>

<a id="transit"></a>

## 10 Transit

You don't need a transit visa if you stay inside HIA between flights <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>. To leave the airport during a stopover, go to a transfer desk, not the Arrivals signs <sup>[199](#o199)</sup>. The residence regulations set a transit visit visa for travellers with an onward ticket, for one trip of 4 to 96 hours (Article 44) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Transit passengers don't need visitor health insurance, but pay the full cost if treated <sup>[181](#o181), [141 (2023)](#o141)</sup>.

<a id="discover-qatar-transit-visa"></a>

### 10.1 Discover Qatar Transit Visa

Travellers who don't qualify for a visa on arrival can apply for it for QR 100 <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>. You need a confirmed Qatar Airways or code-share ticket with at most 96 hours in Doha <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>. There are two routes:

- **Transit under 12 hours:** apply at the Discover Qatar Transit Tour desk at HIA (Duty Free Plaza South, behind the escalators) with a Discover Qatar transit tour booking; the visa is issued immediately <sup>[201](#o201), [200](#o200)</sup>. City and desert tours need a transit of 6 hours or more <sup>[201](#o201), [1](#o1)</sup>.
- **Stopover of 12 to 96 hours:** apply online with a Qatar Stopover hotel booking; it takes 4 working days, and the visa is valid up to 4 days <sup>[200](#o200), [187](#o187), [1](#o1)</sup>. Upload these, each file under 1 MB <sup>[N128](#n128)</sup>:
  - a colour passport copy;
  - a passport valid 3 months or more;
  - a 6 x 6 cm photo on a white background.

<a id="terms"></a>

### 10.2 Terms

If the visa is refused, the fee is refunded less QR 25; once approved it isn't refundable, and it can't be extended beyond 96 hours <sup>[200](#o200)</sup>. MOI decides at its sole discretion, and Discover Qatar refunds the tour if entry is refused although you met the requirements <sup>[201](#o201)</sup>. Hayya's own 4-day transit visa is only through the aviation industry (see [Visas not open to Bangladeshis](#visas-not-open-to-bangladeshis)) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refused transit visa be appealed? <sup>[200](#o200), [201](#o201)</sup>
> - What transit-visa scams are reported? <sup>[200](#o200), [201](#o201)</sup>
> - Does Hayya's no-work rule for its e-visas also cover a transit visa? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>

<a id="student-visa"></a>

## 11 Student visa

Qatar gives a "study residence visa" to a foreign student at a government or private school, institute, college or university in Qatar; it needs a letter from the accredited institution confirming enrolment, a travel document and proof of good conduct (MOI Decision 25 of 2019) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. At Qatar University (QU, the national public university), the student sends the visa documents to the university online and holds the residence permit (RP) under QU's own sponsorship <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>. A QU student already in Qatar on a parent's or company's RP that is being cancelled can apply to move it to QU sponsorship <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>. The student visa covers only the student and gives no right to sponsor family <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

<a id="scholarships"></a>

### 11.1 Scholarships

<a id="qatar-university-undergraduate"></a>

#### 11.1.1 Qatar University (undergraduate)

For scholarship students, QU applies for the visa after the admission decision and tells the student <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>.

- **International Students Scholarship:** awarded competitively during the announced application period, and needs a high-school average of at least 95% <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>. It gives free tuition and textbooks, QR 500 a month, shared student housing with transport, a return air ticket every year and an RP under QU sponsorship <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>.
- **Talent Scholarship:** for non-Qatari undergraduates, international students included, talented in research, technology and innovation, literature, acting (male), media or sport, with a high-school average of at least 80%; it gives free tuition and textbooks and QR 1,000 a month, and international students also get shared housing with transport, a yearly return ticket and an RP under QU sponsorship <sup>[205](#o205)</sup>.

<a id="hbku-graduate-studies"></a>

#### 11.1.2 HBKU (graduate studies)

Applicants are considered for a scholarship during admission, with no separate application, on merit or need and available funds, and awards are not guaranteed <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.

- **Stipend:** international students get QR 9,000 a month for 45 months (PhD) or QR 7,000 a month for 21 months (Master's), paid to full-time students working as research or teaching assistants <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.
- **Tuition:** waived 100% for PhD, 75% for STEM (science, technology, engineering and mathematics) Master's and 60% for humanities and social science Master's; self-funded students get only a need-based rate of QR 2,000 per credit hour <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.
- **Housing:** not paid by HBKU; students pay for a dorm or outside housing from the stipend or their own money <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.
- **Air tickets:** international students get one inbound and one outbound ticket <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What scholarships do the University of Doha for Science and Technology and the other institutions on MOEHE's list offer? <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>

<a id="step-1-check-the-university-and-programme"></a>

### 11.2 Step 1: Check the university and programme

Qatar's Ministry of Education and Higher Education (MOEHE) lists the higher education institutions in Qatar and licenses the private ones and their programmes <sup>[207](#o207), [208](#o208)</sup>. Qatar's Ministry of Foreign Affairs attests a degree only if the university is on MOEHE's list <sup>[104](#o104)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-check-the-university-and-programme-item-1"></a>**Open MOEHE's page** "Higher Education in Qatar" at [www.edu.gov.qa/en/Content/HigherEducationinQatar](https://www.edu.gov.qa/en/Content/HigherEducationinQatar) <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-check-the-university-and-programme-item-2"></a>**Find the institution** under one of two headings; most names link to the institution's own website <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>:
   - "Public Higher Education Institutions": Qatar University, Community College of Qatar, Qatar Aeronautical Academy, University of Doha for Science and Technology, and the military and police academies <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>;
   - "Private Higher Education Institutions" (19, among them Hamad Bin Khalifa University (HBKU), Georgetown University-Qatar, Northwestern University in Qatar, Virginia Commonwealth University in Qatar, Weill Cornell Medical School in Qatar, Carnegie Mellon University in Qatar, Lusail University, and partnership colleges such as City University Qatar (with Ulster University), Oryx University (with Liverpool John Moores) and The National University of Malaysia – Qatar (UKM Qatar)) <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-1-check-the-university-and-programme-item-3"></a>**Download the 2026-2027 programmes list** from the same page and check your exact programme is on it <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How do you report an unlicensed institution or programme to MOEHE? <sup>[207](#o207), [208](#o208)</sup>

<a id="step-2-apply-for-admission"></a>

### 11.3 Step 2: Apply for admission

These steps are for QU <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-2-apply-for-admission-item-1"></a>**Fill in QU's online admission application;** the application fee is not refunded if you aren't accepted <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-2-apply-for-admission-item-2"></a>**Screenshot the application summary page:** the visa application needs it <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.

An IELTS certificate (International English Language Testing System, an English test) is optional for the student visa but may support it <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is QU's admission application fee? <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>
> - What are QU's tuition fees for self-funded international students? <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>
> - What are the visa steps at universities other than QU? <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>

<a id="step-3-prepare-the-visa-documents"></a>

### 11.4 Step 3: Prepare the visa documents

QU's online "Visa Online Application" asks for these <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>:

- **A copy of your passport,** valid at least one year <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.
- **A Financial Sponsorship Declaration** signed by a third-party sponsor (you can't sponsor yourself), with a copy of the sponsor's passport or Qatar ID <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.
- **The sponsor's proof of money:**
  - a sponsor living outside Qatar, for example in Bangladesh, gives a bank statement for the six months before the visa request, in English or Arabic only <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>;
  - a sponsor living in Qatar gives a QR 20,000 (about USD 5,470) bank guarantee from a local Qatari bank in favour of QU, valid 5 years for undergraduate and PhD, 3 years for Master's and 1 year for the Arabic for Non-Native Speakers programme <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.
- **The Health Evaluation form,** filled in and signed by a doctor and stamped by a clinic or hospital in your country of residence <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.
- **A good conduct certificate** from your country of residence <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>: for Bangladesh, the police clearance certificate, obtained as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 2: Screenshot the application summary page](#step-2-apply-for-admission-item-2) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>.
- **Undergraduates: a consent letter** from a parent or guardian to enrol and hold an RP under QU sponsorship <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.
- **The signed Student Declaration Form** <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-submit-the-visa-application-and-wait-for-the-decision"></a>

### 11.5 Step 4: Submit the visa application and wait for the decision

1. <a id="step-4-submit-the-visa-application-and-wait-for-the-decision-item-1"></a>**Submit and upload the documents online** within the timeline QU sets for international students each semester; QU accepts nothing after the published deadline <sup>[203](#o203), [202](#o202)</sup>. Questions go to QU's Scholarships and International Admissions Section, internationalstudents@qu.edu.qa <sup>[202](#o202)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-4-submit-the-visa-application-and-wait-for-the-decision-item-2"></a>**Wait for the committee:** the student visa needs its approval, and an applicant who doesn't meet the student visa requirements isn't considered for admission <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-4-submit-the-visa-application-and-wait-for-the-decision-item-3"></a>**Check the issued visa** by its number on MOI's Visa Inquiry, and print it there, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1](#step-5-after-arrival-complete-the-residence-permit-within-30-days) <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How long does the committee take? <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>
> - Can a refusal be appealed? <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>
> - What is MOI's fee for a student RP? <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>
> - For how long is a student RP issued? <sup>[202](#o202), [203](#o203)</sup>

<a id="step-5-after-arrival-complete-the-residence-permit-within-30-days"></a>

### 11.6 Step 5: After arrival, complete the residence permit within 30 days

Complete the RP within 30 days of entry; a fine runs from day 31 <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

While the RP is valid, keep trips abroad under 180 days in a row; for a longer absence, get an entry permit before 360 days or before the RP expires, whichever comes first <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>.

<a id="after-graduation"></a>

### 11.7 After graduation

QU students may move their RP from QU to a personal sponsor (father, brother, husband) or a company only after graduation; the documents are due within one month after final exams, and the transfer must be finished within 2 months <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>. Students in the Arabic for Non-Native Speakers programme can't transfer and must cancel the RP and leave; a graduate who cancels may stay up to 5 days after receiving the graduation statement <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>. QU accepts no RP transfer or student visa application from a current student with a cumulative GPA below 2.5 <sup>[204](#o204)</sup>. To use a degree for a job, MOEHE runs an online equivalency service (eduservices.edu.gov.qa/Service.aspx?service=UCES) and may refuse degrees that don't meet its rules <sup>[209](#o209)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can job-seeking graduates aged 18 to 28 on university sponsorship still get a one-year visa extension from MOI through Sahem, as reported in 2023? <sup>[N129 (2023)](#n129)</sup>

<a id="can-you-work-3"></a>

### 11.8 Can you work?

A student may not break the purpose of the residence or work for an entity he isn't authorised to work for; breaking this is a crime (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. The authorised routes:

<a id="on-campus"></a>

#### 11.8.1 On campus

QU's Student Employment Program lets enrolled QU students work in QU colleges and departments after 24 credit hours with a GPA (grade point average) of 2.5 or more (not required for Arabic programme and graduate students) <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>, for at most 5 hours a day on weekdays, 7 hours on weekends and holidays, and 20 hours a week <sup>[210 (2023)](#o210)</sup>. At HBKU, stipend holders work 10 hours a week as research assistants and may work at most 20 hours a week in total <sup>[206](#o206)</sup>.

<a id="off-campus-through-sahem"></a>

#### 11.8.2 Off campus through Sahem

Jusour (Qatar Manpower Solutions Company, a state company) runs Sahem, which offers part-time jobs to students at accredited universities in Qatar <sup>[211](#o211)</sup>. You must be a current student at a university or higher education institution in Qatar, aged 17 or older, with a valid Qatar ID; exchange students from universities outside Qatar can't join <sup>[212](#o212)</sup>. You may work at most 20 hours a week during the semester and 40 in the summer holiday, and the part-time contract ends at graduation <sup>[212](#o212)</sup>. Apply on sahem.qa with <sup>[212](#o212)</sup>:
- a copy of the Qatar ID;
- a 6 x 4 passport-size photo;
- a CV;
- a letter of enrolment naming the current semester.

The residence law's permission to work part-time for another employer is written for workers who already have a job <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [212](#o212)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is MOL's rule (hours, permit, fee) for off-campus part-time work outside Sahem by a student on university sponsorship? <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [212](#o212)</sup>

<a id="scams-5"></a>

### 11.9 Scams

<a id="unlicensed-university-or-programme"></a>

#### 11.9.1 Unlicensed "university" or programme

Check as in [Step 1: Check the university and programme](#step-1-check-the-university-and-programme) that the institution and the programme are on MOEHE's lists <sup>[207](#o207)</sup>.

<a id="jobs-outside-the-authorised-routes"></a>

#### 11.9.2 Jobs outside the authorised routes

Work only through the authorised routes (see [Can you work?](#can-you-work-3)); other work is a crime <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="fake-visa-or-fake-sites"></a>

#### 11.9.3 Fake visa or fake sites

Check any issued visa by its number on MOI's Visa Inquiry, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5: After arrival, complete the residence permit within 30 days](#step-5-after-arrival-complete-the-residence-permit-within-30-days) (after item 4), where fake government websites are also covered <sup>[83](#o83), [N93](#n93)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What student-visa scams are reported for Bangladeshis going to Qatar? <sup>[N93](#n93)</sup>

<a id="hayya-a2-a3-and-a4-if-you-live-in-a-gulf-country-hold-certain-visas-or-travel-with-a-gulf-citizen"></a>

## 12 Hayya A2, A3 and A4 (if you live in a Gulf country, hold certain visas or travel with a Gulf citizen)

These Hayya visas are for Bangladeshis who live in a GCC country (A2), hold a valid visa or residence for certain Western countries (A3), or travel with a GCC citizen (A4) <sup>[1](#o1), [52](#o52)</sup>. The A2 and A3 are applied for on Hayya, as in Tourist visa: Hayya A1, [Step 2: Passport, police clearance and certificates](#step-2-passport-police-clearance-and-certificates) <sup>[213](#o213), [178](#o178)</sup>. Both are tourist visas, single entry, valid 30 days, and the A1's rules on insurance, work and overstaying apply <sup>[52](#o52), [132 (2021)](#o132), [192](#o192)</sup>.

<a id="a2-gcc-resident-visa"></a>

### 12.1 A2 (GCC Resident Visa)

The A2 is for Bangladeshis resident in Bahrain, Kuwait, Oman, Saudi Arabia or the UAE, travelling from their country of residence <sup>[1](#o1), [52](#o52)</sup>. It needs a passport and GCC residence ID valid 3 months from arrival, a return ticket if you fly, and QR 100 <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. The residence regulations (Article 39) set the GCC residents' visit visa at one month, extendable to three <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Your profession must be on MOI's list of 206 approved professions (professional and skilled jobs such as specialists, physicians, technicians, businessmen and university students) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [214](#o214), [215 (2023)](#o215)</sup>. On Hayya you pick your job title from its list, matching your residence permit, or choose "others" and type it in <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does Hayya approve an A2 for a profession not on MOI's approved list? <sup>[52](#o52), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Must the GCC residence permit be valid 3 months from the application <sup>[52](#o52)</sup> or 6 months <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>?

GCC residents can still get a visa on arrival <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>; MOI's is single trip, QR 100, 30 days, extendable <sup>[214](#o214)</sup>.

<a id="a3-visa-with-eta-electronic-travel-authorization"></a>

### 12.2 A3 (Visa with ETA, Electronic Travel Authorization)

It needs a valid US, UK, Canada, Schengen, Australia or New Zealand visa or residence at the time of application, a return ticket and QR 100 <sup>[52](#o52), [1](#o1)</sup>. You must hold the authorisation before arriving and be at least 18, unless you come with family, join family in Qatar or are invited by a body in Qatar; if you hold a visa (not residence) from an approved country, you must already have used it in that country (Article 38) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="a4"></a>

### 12.3 A4

The A4 is for companions of GCC citizens, only when travelling with the GCC citizen sponsor; they can't enter alone <sup>[52](#o52), [216](#o216)</sup>.

**If refused:** as for the A1 ([Tourist visa: Hayya A1](#tourist-visa-hayya-a1), If the visa is refused) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is there an appeal after an A2, A3 or A4 refusal? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>
> - What scams are reported for the A2, A3 and A4 visas? <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>

<a id="events-conference-and-medical-visits"></a>

## 13 Events, conference and medical visits

<a id="conference-and-events-visa"></a>

### 13.1 Conference and Events Visa

This is a free Hayya visa for people invited by an event organiser to attend or take part in a conference, event or exhibition; the organiser applies for them, with a photo and a passport valid 3 months from arrival <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. The residence regulations also set event and exhibition or conference visit visas (Articles 37 and 41) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. Hayya has no fixed processing time <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Hayya's 60-day Official Visa is covered separately (see [Business visit](#business-visit)) <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>.

<a id="medical-treatment-visit-visa"></a>

### 13.2 Medical treatment visit visa

This visa is for a visitor coming for treatment at an accredited medical body in Qatar (Article 36) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The host (or a representative) fills in the form, or the visitor applies with a copy of the medical appointment confirmation; it also needs <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>:
- a valid passport;
- the inviting establishment's registration licence;
- a hotel booking or accommodation;
- a return ticket.

Hayya lists no medical visa; its A1 covers tourism and family visits <sup>[52](#o52), [178](#o178)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Where does a Bangladeshi apply for the medical treatment visa? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [52](#o52), [178](#o178)</sup>
> - What is the medical treatment visa's fee? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [52](#o52), [178](#o178)</sup>
> - How long is the medical treatment visa valid? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [52](#o52), [178](#o178)</sup>
> - What is the medical treatment visa's processing time? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [52](#o52), [178](#o178)</sup>
> - Has the medical visa MoPH was reported in April 2026 to be exploring, with an online portal for patients abroad to submit medical reports, launched? <sup>[N130](#n130)</sup>

<a id="treatment-costs"></a>

#### 13.2.1 Treatment costs

Visitors are the first charged MoPH's treatment fees at public HMC and PHCC facilities <sup>[N131 (2023)](#n131)</sup>. Visitor health insurance (MVHI) covers emergencies only ([Tourist visa: Hayya A1](#tourist-visa-hayya-a1), [Step 1: Find a real job](#step-1-find-a-real-job) (Visitor health insurance for the whole stay)) <sup>[181](#o181)</sup>. An international policy that includes Qatar is accepted instead only if it meets MoPH standards, including covering the whole stay <sup>[184 (2022)](#o184), [N131 (2023)](#n131)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a refusal of a conference, event or medical visa be appealed? <sup>[52](#o52), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - What scams are reported for the conference, event and medical visas? <sup>[52](#o52), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>

<a id="permanent-residency-property-and-investor-residence"></a>

## 14 Permanent residency, property and investor residence

<a id="permanent-residency"></a>

### 14.1 Permanent residency

<a id="who-qualifies-2"></a>

#### 14.1.1 Who qualifies

Under Law 10 of 2018 <sup>[145](#o145), [146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>:
- 20 years of legal residence in Qatar (10 if born in Qatar);
- enough income for your family;
- good conduct with no conviction for a dishonourable offence;
- enough Arabic.

The years must be continuous: up to 60 days a year abroad does not break them, but those days are deducted <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>. At most 100 permits are granted a year, unless the Amir approves more <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>.

<a id="what-it-gives"></a>

#### 14.1.2 What it gives

Permanent residency gives treatment and education in government institutions, investment in set sectors without a Qatari partner, and property in designated areas <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>. The holder's spouse and children under 18 share the residence, health and education privileges <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>.

<a id="how-to-apply"></a>

#### 14.1.3 How to apply

MOI's Permanent Residency page has an eligibility inquiry and an online application; contact info@moi.gov.qa <sup>[145](#o145)</sup>. A committee at MOI examines applications and recommends to the Minister of Interior, who decides <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is the permanent residency fee, set by the Minister of Interior's decision? <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>
> - How long is the permanent residency card valid, as set by the Minister of Interior's decision? <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>
> - Can a permanent residency refusal be appealed? <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>
> - May a permanent resident work for any employer? <sup>[146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>

<a id="property-residence"></a>

### 14.2 Property residence

**Thresholds** <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>:
- property worth more than QR 730,000 gives real-estate residency;
- more than QR 3,650,000 gives residency with permanent-residency privileges;
- less gives none.

<a id="where-non-qataris-may-buy"></a>

#### 14.2.1 Where non-Qataris may buy

In 25 areas under Council of Ministers' Decision 28 of 2020: 9 freehold and 16 with a 99-year usufruct (the right to use the property) <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>. The freehold areas are <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>:
- Legtaifiya;
- The Pearl;
- Al Khor resort;
- Lusail;
- Al Kharaej;
- Jabel Thuaileb (Fox Hills);
- Al Dafna (two areas);
- Onaiza.

Outside them, a non-Qatari may own one detached residential unit within a residential complex, and detached shops in commercial complexes, anywhere in Qatar, and may request residency on that basis <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>. A usufruct holder also gets a residence visa <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has the list of areas changed since 2023? <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>

<a id="how-to-apply-1"></a>

#### 14.2.2 How to apply

Submit a purchase request for a foreign investor on the Ministry of Justice website (e-services), with <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>:
- the title deed;
- a copy of your ID or passport;
- if you don't live in Qatar, a good-conduct certificate.

For questions on buying and on real-estate residency, go to the offices for non-Qatari ownership of real estate in the Al Mahara building, The Pearl, or at Qatari Diar Company in Lusail; phone 40216669 or 40216674; NQO@moj.gov.qa <sup>[217 (2023)](#o217)</sup>.

<a id="investor-entrepreneur-talent-and-researcher-residence"></a>

### 14.3 Investor, entrepreneur, talent and researcher residence

The residence regulations set residence visas for these people and their families <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. For the entrepreneur, talent and researcher visas, the concerned state body fills in the form, with its proof of the activity, talent, project or funded research, a valid travel document and proof of good conduct <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

- **Investor:** for investors under the laws on non-Qatari investment, applied for by the concerned state body <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.
- **Entrepreneur or partner:** for founders or partners of businesses and companies in Qatar <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. They, their first-degree dependants and an investor in a start-up or small or medium company worth QR 200,000 get a 3-year permit <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.
- **Talent or temporary project:** for talents in technology, science, creative, sport, cultural or artistic fields, or people running temporary projects in them <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>; talents get a 10-year permit, temporary-project holders 6 months to 1 year, renewable <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.
- **Researcher:** for researchers with grants from national institutions for research carried out for Qatar, with a permit for the length of the research <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.
- **Family:** the spouse, children and parents of these residents, and of property owners and usufruct holders, get their own residence visa <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which state body handles each route? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - What are the fees for each route? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - What are the processing times for each route? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>
> - Have Bangladeshis received these visas? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [217 (2023)](#o217), [146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>
> - Can a refusal (including of property residence) be appealed? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [217 (2023)](#o217), [146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>
> - What scams are reported around permanent, property or investor residence? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [217 (2023)](#o217), [146 (2018)](#o146)</sup>

<a id="changing-visa-type"></a>

## 15 Changing visa type

A foreigner admitted for a set purpose may not break it, leave the sponsor's employment without following the change-of-employer rules, or work for an entity he isn't authorised to work for; doing so is a crime (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>. A work entry permit is granted only under an employment contract with the employer bringing the worker in <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>, and a work residence visa only with that contract certified <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The residence regulations let the authority, in cases it decides, change the purpose of an entry visa inside Qatar while it is still valid <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="visit-tourist-or-business-visa-to-work"></a>

### 15.1 Visit, tourist or business visa to work

A Hayya visitor may not work in Qatar by law <sup>[52](#o52)</sup>. Check any issued visa by its number on MOI's Visa Inquiry, where it can be printed, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 5, item 1: On MOI's Visa Inquiry](#step-5-check-the-visa-yourself-item-1) <sup>[83](#o83)</sup>. Buying or selling visas and working on a "free visa" are illegal (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported); for how the free visa works, see [What ties you to the employer](#what-ties-you-to-the-employer)) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51)</sup>. The 2019 regulations allow a change of purpose at the authority's discretion, MOI's earlier visa FAQ required leaving and re-entering, and Hayya's chief executive said in 2023 that Hayya can't be converted <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [77](#o77), [N62 (2023)](#n62)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a visit visa (Hayya, family visit or business) be changed to a work visa inside Qatar in practice? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [77](#o77), [N62 (2023)](#n62)</sup>
> - What is the fee for changing a visit visa to a work visa inside Qatar? <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [77](#o77), [N62 (2023)](#n62)</sup>

<a id="family-dependant-to-work"></a>

### 15.2 Family dependant to work

Who on family residence may work is covered separately (see [Family residence](#family-residence-spouse-children-and-parents), Can you work?); a husband's residence may go back to the wife when the job ends <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The move goes through MOL's "Join the Labour Market" e-service, or the holder or an employer applies for a work permit <sup>[N126](#n126)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[N126](#n126), [N125](#n125)</sup>:
- a valid QID;
- active residence;
- a registered National Address (the address registered to the QID at any MOI service centre or in Metrash);
- a phone number in your own name.

1. The new employer adds you as a worker, or you enter the new employer's details <sup>[N132 (2023)](#n132)</sup>.
2. MOL decides; after approval the contract is authenticated, the employer pays fees to MOI and your residence changes from family to work <sup>[N126](#n126), [N133 (2023)](#n133)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are MOL's official eligibility rules for moving from family to work residence? <sup>[N126](#n126)</sup>
> - What is MOL's fee for moving from family to work residence? <sup>[N126](#n126)</sup>
> - What documents does MOL officially require for moving from family to work residence? <sup>[N126](#n126)</sup>

<a id="student-to-work"></a>

### 15.3 Student to work

A QU student may move the RP to a company only after graduation, within the deadlines in Student visa, After graduation; students of Arabic for Non-Native Speakers can't transfer <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>. A Sahem part-time contract ends at graduation <sup>[212](#o212)</sup>. The reported Sahem one-year visa extension for job-seeking graduates is covered separately (see [After graduation](#after-graduation)) <sup>[N129 (2023)](#n129)</sup>.

<a id="domestic-worker-to-a-company-job"></a>

### 15.4 Domestic worker to a company job

Any migrant worker may move to another employer under MOL's change-of-employer rules (Decree-Law No. 19 of 2020) <sup>[10 (2020)](#o10)</sup>. A domestic worker who works for the employer's parents, children or spouse is not breaking the rules <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Can a domestic worker move to a company job under the Labour Law, rather than to another household? <sup>[10 (2020)](#o10), [47 (2020)](#o47), [N109](#n109)</sup>
> - How does a domestic worker make that move (see [Ending the job and changing employer](#ending-the-job-and-changing-employer))? <sup>[10 (2020)](#o10), [47 (2020)](#o47), [N109](#n109)</sup>

<a id="company-worker-to-another-company"></a>

### 15.5 Company worker to another company

It keeps the work purpose and needs no consent or NOC from the old employer (see [If you want to change employer](#if-you-want-to-change-employer)) <sup>[10 (2020)](#o10), [172 (2020)](#o172), [11](#o11), [47 (2020)](#o47)</sup>.

<a id="changing-the-job-title-on-the-permit"></a>

### 15.6 Changing the job title on the permit

MOL launched an online service in 2020 for changing the profession (job title) on the residence permit, applied for by the person or the company's authorised signatory with documents <sup>[N134 (2020)](#n134)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are MOL's current rules for changing the profession on the RP? <sup>[N134 (2020)](#n134)</sup>
> - What documents does MOL currently require for changing the profession on the RP? <sup>[N134 (2020)](#n134)</sup>
> - What is MOL's current fee for changing the profession on the RP? <sup>[N134 (2020)](#n134)</sup>

<a id="asylum"></a>

## 16 Asylum

None of the five groups who can get asylum in Qatar covers economic reasons, unpaid wages or a lost job <sup>[218 (2019)](#o218)</sup>. Qatar's political asylum law is Law No. 11 of 2018 <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>, and Qatar is not a party to the 1951 Refugee Convention <sup>[220](#o220)</sup>.

<a id="who-can-get-it"></a>

### 16.1 Who can get it

A "political refugee" is a person outside his own country who cannot or will not return because of a well-founded fear of the death penalty, corporal punishment, torture, cruel or degrading treatment, or persecution because of race, religion, social group or political opinion <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>. Cabinet Decision No. 12 of 2019 limits asylum to five groups:
- human rights defenders facing prosecution or threats for opposing human rights violations <sup>[218 (2019)](#o218)</sup>;
- media correspondents documenting war crimes and crimes against humanity who are pursued or threatened for their work <sup>[218 (2019)](#o218)</sup>;
- members of political parties, religious sects or ethnic minorities facing prosecution or persecution because of that membership <sup>[218 (2019)](#o218)</sup>;
- writers and researchers threatened for opinions they published in newspapers, magazines or blogs <sup>[218 (2019)](#o218)</sup>;
- former or current government officials who oppose or have defected from their governments and fear prosecution <sup>[218 (2019)](#o218)</sup>.

Asylum is refused to anyone who committed a serious non-political crime outside Qatar, a war crime or crime against humanity, or acts against UN purposes, or who has another nationality whose protection is available <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>.

<a id="how-to-apply-2"></a>

### 16.2 How to apply

The application goes to MOI's Human Rights Department, from the applicant, a representative, or through UNHCR (the UN refugee agency), whose office is in the UN House in Lusail <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219), [221](#o221)</sup>. MOI's permanent Committee for Political Refugee Affairs, with members from MOI, Foreign Affairs, Justice, State Security and the National Human Rights Committee, examines it <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>. On its recommendation the Minister may give the applicant temporary residence for three months, renewable, until a decision <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>. A person given an entry visa as an asylum seeker or refugee must complete the medical examination and fingerprints within 15 days of entry <sup>[222 (2019)](#o222)</sup>. The Minister decides within three months of the committee's recommendation; no answer in that time counts as a rejection <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>.

<a id="if-it-is-refused"></a>

### 16.3 If it is refused

You may appeal to the Prime Minister within 30 days of being told; he decides within 30 days, silence counts as rejection, and the decision is final <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>. A holder of temporary residence must leave Qatar within 60 days of being told of the rejection <sup>[222 (2019)](#o222)</sup>. Refugee status is cancelled if obtained with forged documents or fraud <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>.

**What a recognised refugee gets:**
- a travel document, work (except security-related jobs), a monthly allowance until work is found, health care, education and housing <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>;
- a residence card valid two years, renewable <sup>[222 (2019)](#o222)</sup>;
- protection from being sent back to a country where he fears danger or persecution <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>.

He may not take part in political activity in Qatar and may be expelled for doing so <sup>[219 (2018)](#o219)</sup>.

National asylum procedures were still being developed with UNHCR in December 2025 <sup>[223](#o223)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Has any Bangladeshi applied for or received asylum in Qatar? <sup>[223](#o223)</sup>
> - How many people has the committee recognised? <sup>[223](#o223)</sup>

<a id="having-a-child-in-qatar"></a>

## 17 Having a child in Qatar

A baby born in Qatar to Bangladeshi parents does not become Qatari: nationality by origin goes only to children of a Qatari father, and birth in Qatar to foreign parents gives none <sup>[224 (2005)](#o224)</sup>. Naturalisation needs at least 25 consecutive years of lawful residence, among other conditions, and is by Amiri decision <sup>[224 (2005)](#o224)</sup>.

<a id="step-1-get-the-qatari-birth-certificate"></a>

### 17.1 Step 1: Get the Qatari birth certificate

The father if present, the doctor who delivered the baby, hospital directors and the mother must report the birth <sup>[225 (2016)](#o225)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[226 (2024)](#o226)</sup>:
- copies of both parents' valid passports;
- their residence permits, or entry visas if they are not residents;
- the marriage contract attested by the official authorities. A Bangladeshi nikahnama is attested by Bangladesh's Law Ministry and Foreign Ministry and then by the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha, as in Work visa: company worker, [Step 2, item 3](#step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha) <sup>[51 (2021)](#o51), [106](#o106)</sup>.

1. <a id="step-1-get-the-qatari-birth-certificate-item-1"></a>**Report the birth within 15 days** to MoPH's health offices, with the documents above <sup>[225 (2016)](#o225), [226 (2024)](#o226)</sup>.
2. <a id="step-1-get-the-qatari-birth-certificate-item-2"></a>**Choose the name:** it may not be compound or against public order, and full siblings may not share one name <sup>[225 (2016)](#o225)</sup>. For the Bangladeshi registration it needs at least two words (for example "Sakhina Begum", not "Sakhina"), because one-word names cause passport problems abroad <sup>[227 (2023)](#o227)</sup>.
3. <a id="step-1-get-the-qatari-birth-certificate-item-3"></a>**Get the certificate within 7 days of the report** from the competent MOI department; the first original is free <sup>[225 (2016)](#o225)</sup>.

A child born outside marriage or of unknown father is registered only on a court decision (Law No. 1 of 2026) <sup>[228](#o228)</sup>.

An expat guide says applying online is done at eservices.moph.gov.qa/bcmoi with the hospital's Notification of Live Birth or the baby's health card number and the parents' marriage certificate, and extra copies cost QR 20 each <sup>[N135](#n135)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What are MoPH's official steps for applying online?
> - What are MoPH's official fees for applying online?

<a id="step-2-register-the-birth-with-the-bangladesh-embassy-in-doha"></a>

### 17.2 Step 2: Register the birth with the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha

The Embassy registers the births of Bangladeshis born in Qatar, at Building 153, Street 820, Zone 43, New Al-Hilal, Doha; counter hours are under Contacts (see [In Qatar](#in-qatar)) <sup>[229](#o229), [133](#o133)</sup>. It is free within 45 days of birth, then QR 10 up to ten years <sup>[229](#o229)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[230 (2023)](#o230)</sup>:
- the MoPH birth certificate (original and a copy);
- copies of both parents' passports and Qatar IDs;
- a white-background photo of the baby;
- a Bangladeshi mobile number and a personal email address.

1. Go to www.bdris.gov.bd (Bangladesh's online birth registration system) > জন্ম নিবন্ধন (Birth Registration) > জন্ম নিবন্ধন এর জন্য আবেদন করুন (Apply for birth registration) <sup>[230 (2023)](#o230)</sup>.
2. Enter the baby's name of at least two words ([Step 1, item 2: Choose the name](#step-1-get-the-qatari-birth-certificate-item-2)) <sup>[227 (2023)](#o227)</sup>.
3. Type the Qatar address as both the place-of-birth address and the present address <sup>[230 (2023)](#o230)</sup>.
4. Enter your Bangladeshi mobile number and personal email address <sup>[230 (2023)](#o230)</sup>.
5. Upload the MoPH birth certificate and a copy of the father's passport, each as a pdf or jpg of at most 100 KB <sup>[230 (2023)](#o230)</sup>.
6. Print the form and take it to the Embassy counter with the documents above; the Embassy checks them and takes the fee before processing <sup>[230 (2023)](#o230)</sup>. Check the record online at everify.bdris.gov.bd <sup>[231 (2023)](#o231)</sup>.

**If the record is wrong:** apply on bdris.gov.bd under correction <sup>[231 (2023)](#o231)</sup>. A correction needs a working Bangladeshi mobile for the OTP, and the OTP is written on the printed form given to the Embassy <sup>[231 (2023)](#o231)</sup>. Proof of permanent address (a citizenship certificate from the Union Parishad (UP, the village council) chairman, the mayor or a ward councillor) must be notarised in Bangladesh, attested by the Law Ministry and Foreign Ministry, and then attested by the Embassy <sup>[231 (2023)](#o231)</sup>. A correction costs QR 13 and a re-print QR 10 <sup>[229](#o229)</sup>.

<a id="step-3-get-the-baby-a-bangladeshi-e-passport"></a>

### 17.3 Step 3: Get the baby a Bangladeshi e-passport

A newborn applies with the Bangladeshi birth registration certificate and the Qatari birth certificate <sup>[232 (2024)](#o232)</sup>. An under-18 e-passport is valid 5 years with 48 pages <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>. At Bangladesh missions it costs <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>:
- workers and students: USD 30 regular or USD 45 express;
- general applicants: USD 100 regular or USD 150 express.

The Embassy sets the rate by the profession on the applicant's Qatar ID <sup>[232 (2024)](#o232)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which rate (worker/student or general) does the Embassy charge a baby, who has no Qatar ID yet? <sup>[232 (2024)](#o232), [80](#o80)</sup>

1. Apply online at epassport.gov.bd and book an Embassy appointment, regular or express (express gets an earlier slot) <sup>[232 (2024)](#o232)</sup>.
2. As the baby's ID document, enter the online birth registration certificate (English version) <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>.
3. Give the father's or mother's NID number, because the baby has none <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>.
4. In the permanent address column, give a contact address in Bangladesh <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>.
5. Bring a 3R-size lab-print photo with a grey background, required for all applicants under 6 <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>.

   > **Need to know**
   > - What are the photo's dimensions, given the instructions name only the 3R print size? <sup>[93](#o93)</sup>

6. Attend the enrolment in person with the documents; missing the appointment cancels the application, and a new application and appointment are then needed <sup>[232 (2024)](#o232)</sup>.
7. Attend the collection: a signature is taken on delivery <sup>[232 (2024)](#o232)</sup>.

<a id="step-4-apply-for-the-babys-qatar-residence-permit"></a>

### 17.4 Step 4: Apply for the baby's Qatar residence permit

A resident whose family is licensed to live in Qatar must apply for the newborn's RP within 90 days of birth <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. Apply at an MOI Service Centre (Expatriate Affairs), where first-time RPs are issued; MOI's fee is QR 400 <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.

An expat guide says the father applies (at an MOI Service Centre or on Metrash, with the baby's original birth certificate, passport and a blue-background photo) and sponsors if the mother is on his family visa, and the mother sponsors if both parents are on work visas, with an NOC from her sponsor and the father's salary certificate and bank statement <sup>[N136](#n136)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - What is MOI's own document list for a newborn's RP? <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>
> - Does MOI's first-time RP list (form signed by the sponsor, original passport, photo, blood group certificate) apply to a newborn? <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>
> - Who may sponsor the baby?
> - Is the fee QR 400 <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>, or QR 200 for the visa plus QR 500 a year for the QID <sup>[N136](#n136)</sup>?

**A baby born to a mother on a visit visa:** the sponsor must apply within 60 days of birth, and the baby gets an entry (visit) visa if the mother's visa is valid and the baby has a Qatari birth certificate and a travel document <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>.

<a id="if-the-parents-have-no-family-residence"></a>

### 17.5 If the parents have no family residence

The 90-day newborn rule is written for a resident licensed to have his family in Qatar <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>, and family residence needs the sponsor's attested employment contract showing profession and salary, and is granted only for the jobs and salaries listed for family residence (see [Family residence](#family-residence-spouse-children-and-parents), Who qualifies) <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14), [148 (2023)](#o148)</sup>. For a baby born abroad, the regulations accept a mother who is resident and sponsors her family <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. A QU student can't sponsor family members <sup>[203](#o203)</sup>. Unmarried parents: the birth report needs the marriage contract <sup>[226 (2024)](#o226)</sup>, and see [Step 1: Get the Qatari birth certificate](#step-1-get-the-qatari-birth-certificate) for a child born outside marriage <sup>[228](#o228)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - How does a woman company worker or domestic worker without family residence get an RP for a baby born in Qatar? <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [203](#o203)</sup>
> - How does a student get an RP for a baby born in Qatar? <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [203](#o203)</sup>
> - Must the employer or university agree to the baby's RP? <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3), [203](#o203)</sup>

<a id="if-the-baby-is-born-outside-qatar"></a>

### 17.6 If the baby is born outside Qatar

A baby born abroad to a parent with a valid Qatar RP may enter Qatar within six months of birth (the authority may waive this) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>. The residence visa is granted on arrival if the original birth certificate is attested by the Qatari embassy, the baby has not been outside Qatar more than six months since birth, the parents have valid residence (or the mother sponsors her family) and the baby has its own valid travel document <sup>[14 (2019)](#o14)</sup>. The RP fee is the QR 400 in [Step 4: Apply for the baby's Qatar residence permit](#step-4-apply-for-the-babys-qatar-residence-permit) <sup>[122](#o122)</sup>.

<a id="sending-money-home-1"></a>

## 18 Sending money home

<a id="use-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi"></a>

### 18.1 Use a bank or licensed exchange house, never hundi

Bangladesh's government asks workers to send money only through legal channels <sup>[44](#o44)</sup>, and MOI warns against anyone offering "attractive" rates outside approved exchange companies and banks <sup>[N98 (2022)](#n98)</sup>. Exchange houses in Qatar are supervised by QCB's Other Financial Institutions Supervision Department <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>. Money sent through legal channels earns the receiver a 2.5% government cash incentive, sometimes with a further 2.5% from the bank, and the 2026-27 budget keeps the government incentive <sup>[46](#o46), [234](#o234)</sup>.

You'll need:
- For a bKash wallet: the receiver's bKash number and full name exactly as registered <sup>[46](#o46)</sup>.
- For a bank account: your own or a relative's account in Bangladesh <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.

1. <a id="use-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-1"></a>**Choose the channel.** bKash's partners for sending from Qatar are Gulf Exchange, Doha Exchange, Commercial Bank of Qatar, Western Union and MoneyGram <sup>[46](#o46)</sup>. To send straight into a bank account, use a bank or exchange house with a "drawing arrangement" with a Bangladeshi bank <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.
2. <a id="use-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-2"></a>**Send more at once: it costs less.** Sending up to USD 200 from Qatar cost 4.55% on average in January-March and 5.11% in April-June 2026 <sup>[236](#o236)</sup>; across all countries that quarter, 2.94% for up to USD 200, 0.94% for USD 201-500 and 0.50% above USD 500 <sup>[236](#o236)</sup>. In August 2025, sending QR 730 (about USD 200) from Qatar cost 7.18% on average, a QR 20 fee plus a 4.44% exchange-rate margin, and QR 1,820 (about USD 500) 5.54% <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>. For QR 730, each a QR 20 fee plus the margin <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>:
   - Eastern Exchange: 6.72%, 2 days, to a bank account <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>.
   - Ezremit: 6.77%, same day, bank account <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>.
   - Al Dar Exchange: 7.52%, same day, cash pickup <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>.
   - Western Union: 7.70%, online by debit card, under an hour <sup>[237](#o237)</sup>.
3. <a id="use-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-3"></a>**Send.** For bKash, go to a partner branch or agent with the receiver's bKash number and registered name and hand over the cash; up to Tk 2.5 lakh per transfer <sup>[46](#o46)</sup>. Banks in Bangladesh credit remittances within 3 days at most <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>.
4. <a id="use-a-bank-or-licensed-exchange-house-never-hundi-item-4"></a>**The family collects.** bKash money can be cashed out at BRAC Bank and City Bank ATM booths, among others, for Tk 7 per thousand <sup>[46](#o46)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Does money used to buy Wage Earner Development Bonds (see [Saving and pension in Bangladesh](#saving-and-pension-in-bangladesh)) or US Dollar bonds, freelancing income, gifts, trade payments or donations get the 2.5% incentive, when a bank's 2025 guide says not <sup>[N137](#n137)</sup>?
> - Where does a worker check that a Qatar exchange house is licensed by QCB <sup>[233](#o233)</sup>?
> - Which Qatar exchange houses have drawing arrangements with Bangladeshi banks <sup>[235](#o235)</sup>?

**If something goes wrong:**
- QCB consumer protection: 44456412 or 44456456, Qatar Central Bank, Abdullah bin Jasim Street (No. 810), Al Souq, Doha <sup>[238](#o238)</sup>.
- bKash helpline: 16247, or +88-02-55663001 from a country not listed <sup>[46](#o46)</sup>.

<a id="saving-and-pension-in-bangladesh"></a>

### 18.2 Saving and pension in Bangladesh

<a id="nrcta-non-resident-convertible-taka-account"></a>

#### 18.2.1 NRCTA (Non-Resident Convertible Taka Account)

The NRCTA is a taka savings, current or fixed-deposit account for Bangladeshis abroad, funded by remittances, at a scheduled bank's AD (Authorised Dealer, licensed for foreign exchange) branch or Offshore Banking Unit, under Bangladesh Bank's Circular 41 of 8 October 2026 <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>. A worker can open one before departure with no initial deposit, or during a visit home <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>. It earns market interest, and principal and interest can be sent abroad freely <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>. Banks may let someone in Bangladesh operate it under a power of attorney and may send the money to a non-resident nominee if the holder dies, and the account can be kept after coming home for good <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>. Banks may lend in taka against it, including to buy a home for your own use (not for relending, farming or real estate business) <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>. The trade-off: money sent into an NRCTA generally gets no 2.5% incentive unless specifically stated <sup>[239](#o239)</sup>.

<a id="wage-earner-development-bond-wedb"></a>

#### 18.2.2 Wage Earner Development Bond (WEDB)

The WEDB is a 5-year government bond <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.
- Sizes: Tk 25,000, 50,000, 1 lakh, 2 lakh, 5 lakh, 10 lakh and 50 lakh <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.
- Where sold: Bangladesh Bank, any scheduled bank's AD branch, and abroad at branches of Bangladeshi banks, exchange houses and exchange companies <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.
- Who buys: a legal wage earner, in taka, with remittance sent through legal channels, in his own name or a named person's, with no ceiling <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.
- Profit at maturity, paid every six months: 12% for holdings up to Tk 15 lakh, 11% for Tk 15-30 lakh, 10% for Tk 30-50 lakh and 9% above <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.
- Cashing early pays less (up to Tk 15 lakh): 8.70% after 6-12 months, 9.45% after 12-18, 10.20% after 18-24 and 11.20% after 24-60 <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.
- Other terms: profit and investment are income-tax free; you can name a nominee and borrow against the bond; it has a 40-50% death-risk benefit; and the principal can be taken in US dollars <sup>[240](#o240)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Which exchange houses in Qatar sell the WEDB now, given that in 2021 the Embassy named Arabian Exchange Co's main branch, Mushaireb, where buyers brought a salary certificate or contract, valid QID, passport, the nominee's NID or passport copy, and photos <sup>[241 (2021)](#o241)</sup>?

<a id="the-probash-universal-pension"></a>

#### 18.2.3 The Probash universal pension

The National Pension Authority's Universal Pension "Probash" scheme is for Bangladeshis working or living abroad: you pay in foreign currency and the pension is paid in taka <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>. You join at 18 to 50 with your NID or passport number; over 50 only under special consideration, paying 10 years without a break <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>. You pay until 60, and the pension is paid for life <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>. The monthly contribution is Tk 1,000, 2,000, 5,000, 7,500 or 10,000 <sup>[244](#o244)</sup>.

> **Need to know**
> - Is the Tk 1,000 contribution still offered, when the scheme's English brief lists only the other four <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>?

**What you get**
On the scheme's estimates, Tk 1,000 a month for 10 years gives about Tk 1,530 a month, and for 30 years about Tk 12,466 <sup>[244](#o244)</sup>. If you die, your nominee gets the pension until you would have turned 75; if you die before paying 10 years, the deposit comes back with profit <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>. You can borrow up to 50% of your deposit, repaid in up to 24 instalments, and the pension is income-tax free <sup>[242](#o242), [245](#o245)</sup>.

**The rules**
- No lump-sum withdrawal and no leaving before pension age <sup>[245](#o245)</sup>.
- A late contribution has 1 month's grace, then a late fee of 1% a day <sup>[245](#o245)</sup>.
- Never give anyone cash for contributions <sup>[245](#o245)</sup>.
- From Qatar you pay in foreign currency, by credit or debit card, through an exchange house or banking channel, or from the Bangladesh account that receives your remittances, not in taka; contributions sent directly in foreign currency get a government incentive <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243), [245](#o245)</sup>.
- Back home you can pay the same amount in taka, or change scheme <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>.
- You can also open a Surakkha account (the scheme for self-employed and informal workers) for a spouse, parent or sibling aged 18 or over in Bangladesh and pay it in foreign currency, with that person's NID, bank account number and nominee details <sup>[244](#o244), [242](#o242)</sup>.

You'll need <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>:
- Your NID number (or passport number).
- Your Bangladesh bank account name, number and routing number.
- Your nominee's ID number.

1. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-1"></a>**Register** at www.upension.gov.bd: click Registration, choose Probash, and type your NID or passport number, date of birth, mobile number and the captcha <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>. Union Digital Centres (UDC, government service centres at union council level in Bangladesh) help with registration <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>.
2. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-2"></a>**Fill in** occupation, yearly income, permanent address, bank details, and the nominee's ID number, relationship and share; save, check and submit <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>.
3. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-3"></a>**Pay** the first contribution, download the payment slip and keep the email with your pensioner ID <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>.
4. <a id="the-probash-universal-pension-item-4"></a>**Set a password** of at least 6 digits and print the pension ID card <sup>[243 (2024)](#o243)</sup>. If you registered with a passport, submit your NID once you have one <sup>[245](#o245)</sup>.

**Help:** National Pension Authority, 43 Kakrail (4 Anjuman Mufidul Islam Road), Dhaka-1000; hotline 16131 or +88 09610 900800 (9 am-5 pm Bangladesh time); support@upension.gov.bd <sup>[242](#o242)</sup>.

<a id="going-home-without-valid-papers"></a>

## 19 Going home without valid papers

Without a valid passport, a Bangladeshi in Qatar goes home on a Travel Permit (an "outpass", a one-way document home) from the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha <sup>[246](#o246)</sup>. Anyone accused of a residence-law offence, such as overstaying or leaving the employer, can't leave until the case is settled (see [What can get you jailed or deported](#what-can-get-you-jailed-or-deported)) <sup>[3 (2015)](#o3)</sup>.

<a id="help-from-bangladesh"></a>

### 19.1 Help from Bangladesh

A close relative in Bangladesh can ask the Ministry to help bring a worker home; the Ministry acts through the Embassy, WEWB or BMET, free, within 7 working days of receiving the letter <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>. The relative gives <sup>[91](#o91)</sup>:
- Proof that the person is a migrant worker.
- The workplace and residence address in Qatar.
- The recruiting agency's name and address.
- The employer's mobile number.

<a id="step-1-get-a-passport-or-travel-permit"></a>

### 19.2 Step 1: Get a passport or Travel Permit

With a valid passport, go to [Step 2: Settle your residence status](#step-2-settle-your-residence-status). A new e-passport at a Bangladesh mission costs workers USD 30 regular or USD 45 express (48 pages, 5 years) <sup>[80](#o80)</sup>.
